```go
package postgres

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/adapter/postgres/sqlcdb"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/domain"
)

func parseUUID(s string) (uuid.UUID, bool) {
	u, err := uuid.Parse(s)
	return u, err == nil
}

func mustParseUUID(s string) (uuid.UUID, error) {
	u, err := uuid.Parse(s)
	if err != nil {
		return uuid.Nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid uuid %q: %w", s, err)
	}
	return u, nil
}

func newUUID() uuid.UUID { return uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()) }

// toDomainOrder converts a row; items and history are attached by the caller.
func toDomainOrder(r sqlcdb.Order) (domain.Order, error) {
	var addr domain.Address
	if err := json.Unmarshal(r.ShippingAddress, &addr); err != nil {
		return domain.Order{}, fmt.Errorf("decode shipping address of order %s: %w", r.ID, err)
	}
	return domain.Order{
		ID: r.ID.String(), CheckoutID: r.CheckoutID.String(), BuyerID: r.BuyerID.String(),
		ShopID: r.ShopID.String(), ShopName: r.ShopName, SellerUserID: r.SellerUserID.String(),
		Status: domain.Status(r.Status), Subtotal: r.Subtotal, ShippingFee: r.ShippingFee, Total: r.Total,
		ShippingAddress: addr, PaymentMethod: r.PaymentMethod, CancelReason: r.CancelReason,
		Version: int(r.Version), CreatedAt: r.CreatedAt.UTC(), UpdatedAt: r.UpdatedAt.UTC(),
	}, nil
}

func toDomainItem(r sqlcdb.OrderItem) domain.Item {
	return domain.Item{
		SkuID: r.SkuID.String(), ProductID: r.ProductID.String(), ProductName: r.ProductName,
		VariantName: r.VariantName, ImageURL: r.ImageUrl, UnitPrice: r.UnitPrice,
		Quantity: r.Quantity, LineTotal: r.LineTotal,
	}
}

func toDomainHistory(r sqlcdb.OrderStatusHistory) domain.HistoryEntry {
	h := domain.HistoryEntry{To: domain.Status(r.ToStatus), ActorRole: domain.Role(r.ActorRole), Note: r.Note, At: r.CreatedAt.UTC()}
	if r.FromStatus != nil {
		from := domain.Status(*r.FromStatus)
		h.From = &from
	}
	if r.ActorID != nil {
		h.ActorID = r.ActorID.String()
	}
	return h
}

func historyParams(orderID uuid.UUID, h domain.HistoryEntry) (sqlcdb.InsertHistoryParams, error) {
	p := sqlcdb.InsertHistoryParams{
		ID: newUUID(), OrderID: orderID, ToStatus: string(h.To), ActorRole: string(h.ActorRole), Note: h.Note, CreatedAt: h.At,
	}
	if h.From != nil {
		from := string(*h.From)
		p.FromStatus = &from
	}
	if h.ActorID != "" {
		actor, err := mustParseUUID(h.ActorID)
		if err != nil {
			return p, err
		}
		p.ActorID = &actor
	}
	return p, nil
}
```

- [x] **Step 4: Implement the store (reads) and transaction (writes)**

`services/order/internal/adapter/postgres/store.go`:
```go
// Package postgres implements app.Store on PostgreSQL with sqlc-generated
// queries (queries/ → sqlcdb/).
package postgres

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"

	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/pkg/pgxx"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/adapter/postgres/sqlcdb"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/app"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/domain"
)

// Store implements app.Store.
type Store struct {
	pool *pgxpool.Pool
	q    *sqlcdb.Queries
}

var _ app.Store = (*Store)(nil)

// NewStore uses pool, whose database must have the service migrations applied.
func NewStore(pool *pgxpool.Pool) *Store {
	return &Store{pool: pool, q: sqlcdb.New(pool)}
}

func (s *Store) InTx(ctx context.Context, fn func(app.Tx) error) error {
	return pgxx.WithTx(ctx, s.pool, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		return fn(&txStore{tx: tx, q: s.q.WithTx(tx)})
	})
}

func (s *Store) GetOrder(ctx context.Context, id string) (domain.Order, error) {
	uid, ok := parseUUID(id)
	if !ok {
		return domain.Order{}, app.ErrNotFound
	}
	row, err := s.q.GetOrder(ctx, uid)
	if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
		return domain.Order{}, app.ErrNotFound
	}
	if err != nil {
		return domain.Order{}, fmt.Errorf("get order: %w", err)
	}
	orders, err := s.hydrate(ctx, []sqlcdb.Order{row})
	if err != nil {
		return domain.Order{}, err
	}
	return orders[0], nil
}

func (s *Store) ListOrders(ctx context.Context, q app.ListQuery) ([]domain.Order, error) {
	userID, ok := parseUUID(q.UserID)
	if !ok {
		return []domain.Order{}, nil
	}
	var status *string
	if q.Status != nil {
		v := string(*q.Status)
		status = &v
	}
	var beforeAt *time.Time
	var beforeID *uuid.UUID
	if q.After != nil {
		cid, ok := parseUUID(q.After.ID)
		if !ok {
			return nil, app.ErrInvalidCursor
		}
		at := q.After.CreatedAt
		beforeAt, beforeID = &at, &cid
	}
	var rows []sqlcdb.Order
	var err error
	switch q.Party {
	case app.PartyBuyer:
		rows, err = s.q.ListBuyerOrders(ctx, sqlcdb.ListBuyerOrdersParams{
			BuyerID: userID, Status: status, BeforeCreatedAt: beforeAt, BeforeID: beforeID, PageLimit: int32(q.Limit),
		})
	case app.PartySeller:
		rows, err = s.q.ListSellerOrders(ctx, sqlcdb.ListSellerOrdersParams{
			SellerUserID: userID, Status: status, BeforeCreatedAt: beforeAt, BeforeID: beforeID, PageLimit: int32(q.Limit),
		})
	default:
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("list orders: unknown party %d", q.Party)
	}
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("list orders: %w", err)
	}
	return s.hydrate(ctx, rows)
}

// hydrate converts rows and attaches their items and history (two queries).
func (s *Store) hydrate(ctx context.Context, rows []sqlcdb.Order) ([]domain.Order, error) {
	orders := make([]domain.Order, 0, len(rows))
	if len(rows) == 0 {
		return orders, nil
	}
	ids := make([]uuid.UUID, len(rows))
	for i, r := range rows {
		ids[i] = r.ID
	}
	items, err := s.q.ListItemsByOrderIDs(ctx, ids)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("list order items: %w", err)
	}
	history, err := s.q.ListHistoryByOrderIDs(ctx, ids)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("list order history: %w", err)
	}
	itemsBy := map[uuid.UUID][]domain.Item{}
	for _, it := range items {
		itemsBy[it.OrderID] = append(itemsBy[it.OrderID], toDomainItem(it))
	}
	historyBy := map[uuid.UUID][]domain.HistoryEntry{}
	for _, h := range history {
		historyBy[h.OrderID] = append(historyBy[h.OrderID], toDomainHistory(h))
	}
	for _, r := range rows {
		o, err := toDomainOrder(r)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		o.Items, o.History = itemsBy[r.ID], historyBy[r.ID]
		orders = append(orders, o)
	}
	return orders, nil
}
```

`services/order/internal/adapter/postgres/tx.go`:
```go
package postgres

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"time"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"

	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/pkg/event"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/pkg/outbox"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/adapter/postgres/sqlcdb"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/app"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/domain"
)

// txStore implements app.Tx inside one database transaction.
type txStore struct {
	tx pgx.Tx
	q  *sqlcdb.Queries
}

var _ app.Tx = (*txStore)(nil)

func (t *txStore) InsertOrder(ctx context.Context, o domain.Order) error {
	orderID, err := mustParseUUID(o.ID)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	checkoutID, err := mustParseUUID(o.CheckoutID)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	buyerID, err := mustParseUUID(o.BuyerID)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	shopID, err := mustParseUUID(o.ShopID)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	sellerID, err := mustParseUUID(o.SellerUserID)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	addr, err := json.Marshal(o.ShippingAddress)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("encode shipping address: %w", err)
	}
	if err := t.q.InsertOrder(ctx, sqlcdb.InsertOrderParams{
		ID: orderID, CheckoutID: checkoutID, BuyerID: buyerID, ShopID: shopID, ShopName: o.ShopName,
		SellerUserID: sellerID, Status: string(o.Status), Subtotal: o.Subtotal, ShippingFee: o.ShippingFee,
		Total: o.Total, ShippingAddress: addr, PaymentMethod: o.PaymentMethod, CancelReason: o.CancelReason,
		Version: int32(o.Version), CreatedAt: o.CreatedAt, UpdatedAt: o.UpdatedAt,
	}); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("insert order: %w", err)
	}
	for _, it := range o.Items {
		skuID, err := mustParseUUID(it.SkuID)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		productID, err := mustParseUUID(it.ProductID)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if err := t.q.InsertOrderItem(ctx, sqlcdb.InsertOrderItemParams{
			ID: newUUID(), OrderID: orderID, SkuID: skuID, ProductID: productID, ProductName: it.ProductName,
			VariantName: it.VariantName, ImageUrl: it.ImageURL, UnitPrice: it.UnitPrice,
			Quantity: it.Quantity, LineTotal: it.LineTotal,
		}); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("insert order item: %w", err)
		}
	}
	for _, h := range o.History {
		p, err := historyParams(orderID, h)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if err := t.q.InsertHistory(ctx, p); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("insert order history: %w", err)
		}
	}
	return nil
}

func (t *txStore) SaveTransition(ctx context.Context, o domain.Order, prevVersion int) error {
	if len(o.History) == 0 {
		return errors.New("save transition: order has no history entry")
	}
	id, err := mustParseUUID(o.ID)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	n, err := t.q.UpdateOrderStatus(ctx, sqlcdb.UpdateOrderStatusParams{
		Status: string(o.Status), CancelReason: o.CancelReason, UpdatedAt: o.UpdatedAt, ID: id, Version: int32(prevVersion),
	})
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("update order status: %w", err)
	}
	if n == 0 {
		return app.ErrConcurrentUpdate
	}
	p, err := historyParams(id, o.History[len(o.History)-1])
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if err := t.q.InsertHistory(ctx, p); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("insert order history: %w", err)
	}
	return nil
}

func (t *txStore) Publish(ctx context.Context, orderID string, p event.Payload, at time.Time) error {
	b, err := event.Encode(p, at)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return outbox.Write(ctx, t.tx, outbox.Message{Topic: event.TopicOrderEvents, Key: orderID, Payload: b})
}

func (t *txStore) ClaimStalePending(ctx context.Context, before time.Time, limit int) ([]domain.Order, error) {
	rows, err := t.q.ClaimStalePending(ctx, sqlcdb.ClaimStalePendingParams{CreatedBefore: before, BatchSize: int32(limit)})
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("claim stale pending orders: %w", err)
	}
	orders := make([]domain.Order, 0, len(rows))
	for _, r := range rows {
		o, err := toDomainOrder(r)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		orders = append(orders, o)
	}
	return orders, nil
}
```

- [x] **Step 5: Implement idempotency storage**

`services/order/internal/adapter/postgres/idempotency.go`:
```go
package postgres

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"time"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"

	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/adapter/postgres/sqlcdb"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/app"
)

func (s *Store) BeginIdempotency(ctx context.Context, userID, key string, requestHash []byte, at time.Time) (app.IdempotencyRecord, bool, error) {
	uid, err := mustParseUUID(userID)
	if err != nil {
		return app.IdempotencyRecord{}, false, err
	}
	n, err := s.q.InsertIdempotencyKey(ctx, sqlcdb.InsertIdempotencyKeyParams{UserID: uid, Key: key, RequestHash: requestHash, CreatedAt: at})
	if err != nil {
		return app.IdempotencyRecord{}, false, fmt.Errorf("insert idempotency key: %w", err)
	}
	if n == 1 {
		return app.IdempotencyRecord{RequestHash: requestHash}, true, nil
	}
	row, err := s.q.GetIdempotencyKey(ctx, sqlcdb.GetIdempotencyKeyParams{UserID: uid, Key: key})
	if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
		// The request that owned the key failed and released it between our
		// insert and this read; report "in progress" so the client retries.
		return app.IdempotencyRecord{RequestHash: requestHash}, false, nil
	}
	if err != nil {
		return app.IdempotencyRecord{}, false, fmt.Errorf("get idempotency key: %w", err)
	}
	rec := app.IdempotencyRecord{RequestHash: row.RequestHash}
	if row.StatusCode != nil {
		rec.Response = &app.Response{Status: int(*row.StatusCode), Body: row.Response}
	}
	return rec, false, nil
}

func (s *Store) DeleteIdempotencyKey(ctx context.Context, userID, key string) error {
	uid, err := mustParseUUID(userID)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if err := s.q.DeleteIdempotencyKey(ctx, sqlcdb.DeleteIdempotencyKeyParams{UserID: uid, Key: key}); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("delete idempotency key: %w", err)
	}
	return nil
}

func (s *Store) DeleteIdempotencyKeysBefore(ctx context.Context, before time.Time) (int64, error) {
	n, err := s.q.DeleteIdempotencyKeysBefore(ctx, before)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("delete expired idempotency keys: %w", err)
	}
	return n, nil
}

func (t *txStore) CompleteIdempotencyKey(ctx context.Context, userID, key string, resp app.Response) error {
	uid, err := mustParseUUID(userID)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	code := int32(resp.Status)
	if err := t.q.CompleteIdempotencyKey(ctx, sqlcdb.CompleteIdempotencyKeyParams{
		Response: resp.Body, StatusCode: &code, UserID: uid, Key: key,
	}); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("complete idempotency key: %w", err)
	}
	return nil
}
```

```go
package redisadapter_test

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"testing"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	"github.com/redis/go-redis/v9"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"

)

var ctx = context.Background()

func id() string { return uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()).String() }

func TestCartStore(t *testing.T) {
	opts, err := redis.ParseURL(testutil.Redis(t))
	require.NoError(t, err)
	rdb := redis.NewClient(opts)
	t.Cleanup(func() { _ = rdb.Close() })
	s := redisadapter.NewCartStore(rdb)

	t.Run("empty cart", func(t *testing.T) {
		lines, err := s.Lines(ctx, id())
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.NotNil(t, lines)
		assert.Empty(t, lines)
	})

	t.Run("set, update and read lines in the spec's key layout", func(t *testing.T) {
		user, a, b := id(), id(), id()
		require.NoError(t, s.Set(ctx, user, a, 2))
		require.NoError(t, s.Set(ctx, user, b, 1))
		require.NoError(t, s.Set(ctx, user, a, 5))

		lines, err := s.Lines(ctx, user)
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.Equal(t, map[string]int32{a: 5, b: 1}, lines)
		raw, err := rdb.HGet(ctx, "cart:"+user, a).Result()
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.Equal(t, "5", raw)
		ttl, err := rdb.TTL(ctx, "cart:"+user).Result()
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.Equal(t, int64(-1), int64(ttl), "no TTL")
	})

	t.Run("remove lines, unknown ones and none are fine", func(t *testing.T) {
		user, a, b := id(), id(), id()
		require.NoError(t, s.Set(ctx, user, a, 1))
		require.NoError(t, s.Set(ctx, user, b, 1))
		require.NoError(t, s.Remove(ctx, user, a, id()))
		require.NoError(t, s.Remove(ctx, user))
		lines, err := s.Lines(ctx, user)
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.Equal(t, map[string]int32{b: 1}, lines)
	})

	t.Run("carts are per user", func(t *testing.T) {
		u1, u2, sku := id(), id(), id()
		require.NoError(t, s.Set(ctx, u1, sku, 3))
		lines, err := s.Lines(ctx, u2)
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.Empty(t, lines)
	})

	t.Run("at most 50 distinct lines, updates always allowed", func(t *testing.T) {
		user := id()
		var skus []string
		for i := range domain.MaxCartLines {
			skus = append(skus, id())
			require.NoError(t, s.Set(ctx, user, skus[i], 1), fmt.Sprint(i))
		}
		assert.ErrorIs(t, s.Set(ctx, user, id(), 1), app.ErrCartFull)
		require.NoError(t, s.Set(ctx, user, skus[0], 9), "updating an existing line in a full cart")
		require.NoError(t, s.Remove(ctx, user, skus[1]))
		require.NoError(t, s.Set(ctx, user, id(), 1), "room again after a removal")
		lines, err := s.Lines(ctx, user)
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.Len(t, lines, domain.MaxCartLines)
	})
}
```

- [x] **Step 4: Implement the cart store**

`services/order/internal/adapter/redis/cartstore.go`:
```go
// Package redisadapter stores carts in Redis as a hash per user:
// key cart:{user_id}, field sku_id, value quantity, no TTL (WS3 §2).
package redisadapter

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"strconv"

	"github.com/redis/go-redis/v9"

	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/app"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/domain"
)

// CartStore implements app.CartStore.
type CartStore struct {
	rdb *redis.Client
}

var _ app.CartStore = (*CartStore)(nil)

// NewCartStore uses rdb.
func NewCartStore(rdb *redis.Client) *CartStore { return &CartStore{rdb: rdb} }

// setLine writes ARGV[1]=ARGV[2] into hash KEYS[1] unless it would add a new
// field beyond ARGV[3] fields. Atomic, so concurrent adds cannot exceed the cap.
var setLine = redis.NewScript(`
if redis.call('HEXISTS', KEYS[1], ARGV[1]) == 1 or redis.call('HLEN', KEYS[1]) < tonumber(ARGV[3]) then
  redis.call('HSET', KEYS[1], ARGV[1], ARGV[2])
  return 1
end
return 0
`)

func cartKey(userID string) string { return "cart:" + userID }

func (c *CartStore) Lines(ctx context.Context, userID string) (map[string]int32, error) {
	raw, err := c.rdb.HGetAll(ctx, cartKey(userID)).Result()
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("read cart: %w", err)
	}
	lines := make(map[string]int32, len(raw))
	for sku, v := range raw {
		q, err := strconv.ParseInt(v, 10, 32)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("cart of %s: bad quantity %q for sku %s", userID, v, sku)
		}
		lines[sku] = int32(q)
	}
	return lines, nil
}

func (c *CartStore) Set(ctx context.Context, userID, skuID string, quantity int32) error {
	ok, err := setLine.Run(ctx, c.rdb, []string{cartKey(userID)}, skuID, quantity, domain.MaxCartLines).Int()
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("set cart line: %w", err)
	}
	if ok == 0 {
		return app.ErrCartFull
	}
	return nil
}

func (c *CartStore) Remove(ctx context.Context, userID string, skuIDs ...string) error {
	if len(skuIDs) == 0 {
		return nil
	}
	if err := c.rdb.HDel(ctx, cartKey(userID), skuIDs...).Err(); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("remove cart lines: %w", err)
	}
	return nil
}
```

```go
	buyerID = "01928c3a-0000-7000-8000-000000000002"
)

func TestTransitionEventsForSystemChangeOmitActorID(t *testing.T) {
	o := domain.Order{ID: orderID}
	evs := transitionEvents(o, domain.StatusChange{From: domain.StatusPending, To: domain.StatusPendingConfirmation, Actor: domain.SystemActor()})
	assert.Equal(t, []event.Payload{event.OrderStatusChanged{OrderID: orderID, From: "PENDING", To: "PENDING_CONFIRMATION", ActorRole: "system"}}, evs)
}

func TestTransitionEventsForCancellationAddOrderCancelled(t *testing.T) {
	reason := "đổi ý"
	o := domain.Order{ID: orderID, CancelReason: &reason}
	buyer := domain.Actor{ID: buyerID, Role: domain.RoleBuyer}
	evs := transitionEvents(o, domain.StatusChange{From: domain.StatusPendingConfirmation, To: domain.StatusCancelled, Actor: buyer})
	assert.Equal(t, []event.Payload{
		event.OrderStatusChanged{OrderID: orderID, From: "PENDING_CONFIRMATION", To: "CANCELLED", ActorID: buyerID, ActorRole: "buyer"},
		event.OrderCancelled{OrderID: orderID, Reason: reason, CancelledBy: "buyer"},
	}, evs)
}

func TestEventsMatchTheContractSchemas(t *testing.T) {
	c := jsonschema.NewCompiler()
	c.AssertFormat()
	dir := filepath.Join("..", "..", "..", "..", "api", "events")
	schemas := map[event.Type]*jsonschema.Schema{}
	for typ, file := range map[event.Type]string{
		event.TypeOrderCreated:       "order-created.schema.json",
		event.TypeOrderStatusChanged: "order-status-changed.schema.json",
		event.TypeOrderCancelled:     "order-cancelled.schema.json",
	} {
		sch, err := c.Compile(filepath.Join(dir, file))
		require.NoError(t, err)
		schemas[typ] = sch
	}

	reason := domain.CancelCheckoutTimeout
	o := domain.Order{
		ID: orderID, CheckoutID: orderID, BuyerID: buyerID, ShopID: buyerID, SellerUserID: buyerID, Total: 130_000,
		Items: []domain.Item{{SkuID: orderID, Quantity: 1, UnitPrice: 100_000}}, CancelReason: &reason,
	}
	payloads := append([]event.Payload{orderCreated(o)},
		transitionEvents(o, domain.StatusChange{From: domain.StatusPending, To: domain.StatusCancelled, Actor: domain.SystemActor()})...)
	payloads = append(payloads, transitionEvents(o, domain.StatusChange{From: domain.StatusConfirmed, To: domain.StatusCancelled, Actor: domain.Actor{ID: buyerID, Role: domain.RoleSeller}})...)
	for _, p := range payloads {
		b, err := event.Encode(p, time.Now())
		require.NoError(t, err)
		inst, err := jsonschema.UnmarshalJSON(bytes.NewReader(b))
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.NoError(t, schemas[p.EventType()].Validate(inst), string(b))
	}
}
```

```go
package app_test

import (

	"testing"

	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"

)

func TestCursorRoundTrip(t *testing.T) {
	c := app.Cursor{CreatedAt: time.Date(2026, 9, 28, 10, 0, 0, 123456000, time.UTC), ID: "01928c3a-0000-7000-8000-000000000001"}
	got, err := app.DecodeCursor(app.EncodeCursor(c))
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, c, got)
}

func TestDecodeCursorRejectsGarbage(t *testing.T) {
	enc := func(s string) string { return base64.RawURLEncoding.EncodeToString([]byte(s)) }
	for _, s := range []string{
		"!!!", enc("no-separator"), enc("abc|01928c3a-0000-7000-8000-000000000001"), enc("123|not-a-uuid"),
	} {
		_, err := app.DecodeCursor(s)
		assert.ErrorIs(t, err, app.ErrInvalidCursor, s)
	}
}
```

`services/order/internal/app/env_test.go`:
```go
package app_test

import (
	"context"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"
	"github.com/redis/go-redis/v9"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"

	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/pkg/event"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/pkg/pgxx"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/pkg/testutil"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/adapter/catalog"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/adapter/catalog/catalogfake"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/adapter/postgres"
	redisadapter "github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/adapter/redis"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/app"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/domain"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/migrations"
)

var ctx = context.Background()

var testAddress = domain.Address{FullName: "Nguyễn Văn A", Phone: "0900000000", Line1: "1 Lê Lợi", Ward: "Bến Nghé", District: "Quận 1", Province: "TP.HCM"}

func uid() string { return uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()).String() }

// clock is a controllable app clock (UTC, µs precision like Postgres).
type clock struct {
	mu sync.Mutex
	t  time.Time
}

func newClock() *clock { return &clock{t: time.Now().UTC().Truncate(time.Microsecond)} }

func (c *clock) Now() time.Time {
	c.mu.Lock()
	defer c.mu.Unlock()
	return c.t
}

func (c *clock) Advance(d time.Duration) {
	c.mu.Lock()
	defer c.mu.Unlock()
	c.t = c.t.Add(d)
}

type envOpts struct{ postgres, redis bool }

// env is the real adapters wired to throwaway containers and the catalog fake.
type env struct {
	store   *postgres.Store
	pool    *pgxpool.Pool
	rdb     *redis.Client
	carts   *redisadapter.CartStore
	fake    *catalogfake.Fake
	catalog *catalog.Client
	clock   *clock
}

func newEnv(t *testing.T, o envOpts) *env {
	e := &env{fake: catalogfake.New(), clock: newClock()}
	e.catalog = catalog.New(e.fake.Dial(t), catalog.Config{Timeout: 300 * time.Millisecond, Retries: 2, Backoff: 5 * time.Millisecond})
	if o.postgres {
		dsn := testutil.Postgres(t)
		require.NoError(t, pgxx.Migrate(dsn, migrations.FS))
		pool, err := pgxx.NewPool(ctx, dsn)
		require.NoError(t, err)
		t.Cleanup(pool.Close)
		e.pool, e.store = pool, postgres.NewStore(pool)
	}
	if o.redis {
		opts, err := redis.ParseURL(testutil.Redis(t))
		require.NoError(t, err)
		e.rdb = redis.NewClient(opts)
		t.Cleanup(func() { _ = e.rdb.Close() })
		e.carts = redisadapter.NewCartStore(e.rdb)
	}
	return e
}

func (e *env) cartSet(t *testing.T, user, skuID string, q int32) {
	t.Helper()
	require.NoError(t, e.carts.Set(ctx, user, skuID, q))
}

// events returns the outbox events of orderID in write order.
func (e *env) events(t *testing.T, orderID string) []event.Envelope {
	t.Helper()
	rows, err := e.pool.Query(ctx, `SELECT payload FROM outbox WHERE msg_key = $1 ORDER BY created_at, id`, orderID)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	defer rows.Close()
	var out []event.Envelope
	for rows.Next() {
		var b []byte
		require.NoError(t, rows.Scan(&b))
		env, err := event.Decode(b)
		require.NoError(t, err)
		out = append(out, env)
	}
	require.NoError(t, rows.Err())
	return out
}

func types(evs []event.Envelope) []event.Type {
	out := make([]event.Type, len(evs))
	for i, e := range evs {
		out[i] = e.EventType
	}
	return out
}

// seedOrder stores a one-item order of buyer at shop and walks it along the
// happy path until it reaches status (no outbox events are written).
func (e *env) seedOrder(t *testing.T, buyer string, shop catalogfake.Shop, status domain.Status) domain.Order {
	t.Helper()
	skuID := uid()
	orders, err := domain.BuildOrders(domain.CheckoutParams{
		CheckoutID: uid(), BuyerID: buyer, Lines: []domain.Line{{SkuID: skuID, Quantity: 1}},
		Skus: map[string]domain.Sku{skuID: {
			ID: skuID, ProductID: uid(), ProductName: "Áo", VariantName: "M", Price: 10_000, Stock: 5, Available: true,
			ShopID: shop.ID, ShopName: shop.Name, ShopSlug: shop.Slug, SellerUserID: shop.SellerUserID,
		}},
		ShippingFee: 30_000, Address: testAddress, PaymentMethod: domain.PaymentCOD, Now: e.clock.Now(), NewID: uid,
	})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	o := orders[0]
	require.NoError(t, e.store.InTx(ctx, func(tx app.Tx) error { return tx.InsertOrder(ctx, o) }))

	path := []struct {
		action domain.Action
		actor  domain.Actor
	}{
		{domain.ActionSubmit, domain.SystemActor()},
		{domain.ActionConfirm, domain.Actor{ID: shop.SellerUserID, Role: domain.RoleSeller}},
		{domain.ActionShip, domain.Actor{ID: shop.SellerUserID, Role: domain.RoleSeller}},
		{domain.ActionDeliver, domain.Actor{ID: shop.SellerUserID, Role: domain.RoleSeller}},
		{domain.ActionComplete, domain.Actor{ID: buyer, Role: domain.RoleBuyer}},
	}
	for _, step := range path {
		if o.Status == status {
			break
		}
		ch, err := domain.Transition(o, step.action, step.actor)
		require.NoError(t, err)
		prev := o.Version
		o.Apply(ch, "", e.clock.Now())
		require.NoError(t, e.store.InTx(ctx, func(tx app.Tx) error { return tx.SaveTransition(ctx, o, prev) }))
		o.Version = prev + 1
	}
	require.Equal(t, status, o.Status, "seedOrder only walks the happy path")
	return o
}
```

```go
package app_test

import (

	"slices"

)

func TestOrderService(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t, envOpts{postgres: true})
	svc := app.NewOrderService(e.store, e.clock.Now)

	t.Run("get is visible to buyer and seller only", func(t *testing.T) {
		buyer, shop := uid(), catalogfake.NewShop("Shop Get")
		o := e.seedOrder(t, buyer, shop, domain.StatusPendingConfirmation)
		for _, who := range []string{buyer, shop.SellerUserID} {
			got, err := svc.Get(ctx, who, o.ID)
			require.NoError(t, err)
			assert.Equal(t, o, got)
		}
		_, err := svc.Get(ctx, uid(), o.ID)
		assert.ErrorIs(t, err, app.ErrNotFound)
		_, err = svc.Get(ctx, buyer, uid())
		assert.ErrorIs(t, err, app.ErrNotFound)
	})

	t.Run("happy path to completed writes history and events", func(t *testing.T) {
		buyer, shop := uid(), catalogfake.NewShop("Shop Life")
		seller := domain.Actor{ID: shop.SellerUserID, Role: domain.RoleSeller}
		o := e.seedOrder(t, buyer, shop, domain.StatusPendingConfirmation)
		for _, step := range []struct {
			action domain.Action
			actor  domain.Actor
			want   domain.Status
		}{
			{domain.ActionConfirm, seller, domain.StatusConfirmed},
			{domain.ActionShip, seller, domain.StatusShipping},
			{domain.ActionDeliver, seller, domain.StatusDelivered},
			{domain.ActionComplete, domain.Actor{ID: buyer, Role: domain.RoleBuyer}, domain.StatusCompleted},
		} {
			e.clock.Advance(time.Second)
			got, err := svc.Act(ctx, app.ActInput{OrderID: o.ID, Actor: step.actor, Action: step.action, Note: "ok"})
			require.NoError(t, err)
			assert.Equal(t, step.want, got.Status)
		}
		final, err := svc.Get(ctx, buyer, o.ID)
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.Equal(t, domain.StatusCompleted, final.Status)
		assert.Len(t, final.History, 6)
		assert.Equal(t, "ok", *final.History[5].Note)

		evs := e.events(t, o.ID)
		assert.Equal(t, slices.Repeat([]event.Type{event.TypeOrderStatusChanged}, 4), types(evs))
		first, err := event.DecodeData[event.OrderStatusChanged](evs[0])
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.Equal(t, event.OrderStatusChanged{OrderID: o.ID, From: "PENDING_CONFIRMATION", To: "CONFIRMED", ActorID: shop.SellerUserID, ActorRole: "seller"}, first)
	})

	t.Run("buyer cancel while pending confirmation emits OrderCancelled", func(t *testing.T) {
		buyer := uid()
		o := e.seedOrder(t, buyer, catalogfake.NewShop("Shop Cancel"), domain.StatusPendingConfirmation)
		got, err := svc.Act(ctx, app.ActInput{OrderID: o.ID, Actor: domain.Actor{ID: buyer, Role: domain.RoleBuyer}, Action: domain.ActionCancel, Note: "đổi ý"})
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.Equal(t, domain.StatusCancelled, got.Status)
		assert.Equal(t, "đổi ý", *got.CancelReason)

		evs := e.events(t, o.ID)
		require.Equal(t, []event.Type{event.TypeOrderStatusChanged, event.TypeOrderCancelled}, types(evs))
		c, err := event.DecodeData[event.OrderCancelled](evs[1])
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.Equal(t, event.OrderCancelled{OrderID: o.ID, Reason: "đổi ý", CancelledBy: "buyer"}, c)
	})

	t.Run("buyer cannot cancel a confirmed order but its seller can", func(t *testing.T) {
		buyer, shop := uid(), catalogfake.NewShop("Shop Confirmed")
		o := e.seedOrder(t, buyer, shop, domain.StatusConfirmed)
		_, err := svc.Act(ctx, app.ActInput{OrderID: o.ID, Actor: domain.Actor{ID: buyer, Role: domain.RoleBuyer}, Action: domain.ActionCancel, Note: "x"})
		assert.ErrorIs(t, err, domain.ErrInvalidTransition)
		got, err := svc.Act(ctx, app.ActInput{OrderID: o.ID, Actor: domain.Actor{ID: shop.SellerUserID, Role: domain.RoleSeller}, Action: domain.ActionCancel, Note: "hết hàng"})
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.Equal(t, domain.StatusCancelled, got.Status)
	})

	t.Run("strangers get not found", func(t *testing.T) {
		buyer, shop := uid(), catalogfake.NewShop("Shop Owner")
		o := e.seedOrder(t, buyer, shop, domain.StatusPendingConfirmation)
		_, err := svc.Act(ctx, app.ActInput{OrderID: o.ID, Actor: domain.Actor{ID: uid(), Role: domain.RoleSeller}, Action: domain.ActionConfirm})
		assert.ErrorIs(t, err, app.ErrNotFound, "another shop's seller")
		_, err = svc.Act(ctx, app.ActInput{OrderID: o.ID, Actor: domain.Actor{ID: shop.SellerUserID, Role: domain.RoleBuyer}, Action: domain.ActionCancel, Note: "x"})
		assert.ErrorIs(t, err, app.ErrNotFound, "the seller is not the buyer")
	})

	t.Run("concurrent confirm and cancel: exactly one wins", func(t *testing.T) {
		for range 20 {
			buyer, shop := uid(), catalogfake.NewShop("Shop Race")
			o := e.seedOrder(t, buyer, shop, domain.StatusPendingConfirmation)
			inputs := []app.ActInput{
				{OrderID: o.ID, Actor: domain.Actor{ID: shop.SellerUserID, Role: domain.RoleSeller}, Action: domain.ActionConfirm},
				{OrderID: o.ID, Actor: domain.Actor{ID: buyer, Role: domain.RoleBuyer}, Action: domain.ActionCancel, Note: "đổi ý"},
			}
			errs := make([]error, len(inputs))
			start := make(chan struct{})
			var wg sync.WaitGroup
			for i, in := range inputs {
				wg.Add(1)
				go func() {
					defer wg.Done()
					<-start
					_, errs[i] = svc.Act(ctx, in)
				}()
			}
			close(start)
			wg.Wait()

			wins := 0
			for _, err := range errs {
				if err == nil {
					wins++
					continue
				}
				assert.True(t, errors.Is(err, domain.ErrInvalidTransition) || errors.Is(err, app.ErrConcurrentUpdate), "unexpected error: %v", err)
			}
			assert.Equal(t, 1, wins)
			got, err := svc.Get(ctx, buyer, o.ID)
			require.NoError(t, err)
			assert.Len(t, got.History, 3)
			assert.Len(t, e.events(t, o.ID), map[domain.Status]int{domain.StatusConfirmed: 1, domain.StatusCancelled: 2}[got.Status])
		}
	})

	t.Run("list pages newest first with cursors", func(t *testing.T) {
		buyer, shop := uid(), catalogfake.NewShop("Shop List")
		var want []string
		for range 5 {
			e.clock.Advance(time.Second)
			want = append(want, e.seedOrder(t, buyer, shop, domain.StatusPendingConfirmation).ID)
		}
		slices.Reverse(want)

		var got []string
		cursor, pages := "", 0
		for {
			page, err := svc.List(ctx, app.ListInput{Party: app.PartyBuyer, UserID: buyer, Limit: 2, Cursor: cursor})
			require.NoError(t, err)
			pages++
			for _, o := range page.Orders {
				got = append(got, o.ID)
			}
			if page.NextCursor == nil {
				break
			}
			cursor = *page.NextCursor
		}
		assert.Equal(t, want, got)
		assert.Equal(t, 3, pages)

		confirmed := domain.StatusConfirmed
		page, err := svc.List(ctx, app.ListInput{Party: app.PartySeller, UserID: shop.SellerUserID, Status: &confirmed, Limit: 20})
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.Empty(t, page.Orders)
		assert.Nil(t, page.NextCursor)

		_, err = svc.List(ctx, app.ListInput{Party: app.PartyBuyer, UserID: buyer, Limit: 2, Cursor: "garbage"})
		assert.ErrorIs(t, err, app.ErrInvalidCursor)
	})
}
```

- [x] **Step 7: Implement transition persistence**

`services/order/internal/app/transition.go`:
```go
package app

import (
	"context"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/pkg/event"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/domain"
)

// applyTransition moves o through action by actor and persists it in tx:
// status (optimistic lock on o.Version), a history row and the outbox events.
// On success o reflects the stored state, including its new version.
func applyTransition(ctx context.Context, tx Tx, o *domain.Order, action domain.Action, actor domain.Actor, note string, at time.Time) error {
	ch, err := domain.Transition(*o, action, actor)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	prev := o.Version
	o.Apply(ch, note, at)
	if err := tx.SaveTransition(ctx, *o, prev); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	o.Version = prev + 1
	for _, p := range transitionEvents(*o, ch) {
		if err := tx.Publish(ctx, o.ID, p, at); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// transitionEvents returns the events of a status change: always
// OrderStatusChanged, plus OrderCancelled for cancellations (catalog releases
// the order's stock when it consumes it).
func transitionEvents(o domain.Order, ch domain.StatusChange) []event.Payload {
	evs := []event.Payload{event.OrderStatusChanged{
		OrderID: o.ID, From: string(ch.From), To: string(ch.To), ActorID: ch.Actor.ID, ActorRole: string(ch.Actor.Role),
	}}
	if ch.To == domain.StatusCancelled {
		reason := ""
		if o.CancelReason != nil {
			reason = *o.CancelReason
		}
		evs = append(evs, event.OrderCancelled{OrderID: o.ID, Reason: reason, CancelledBy: string(ch.Actor.Role)})
	}
	return evs
}

func orderCreated(o domain.Order) event.OrderCreated {
	items := make([]event.OrderItem, len(o.Items))
	for i, it := range o.Items {
		items[i] = event.OrderItem{SkuID: it.SkuID, Quantity: it.Quantity, UnitPrice: it.UnitPrice}
	}
	return event.OrderCreated{
		OrderID: o.ID, CheckoutID: o.CheckoutID, BuyerID: o.BuyerID, ShopID: o.ShopID,
		SellerUserID: o.SellerUserID, Total: o.Total, Items: items,
	}
}

func newID() string { return uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()).String() }

// defaultNow is the service clock: UTC, truncated to Postgres' precision.
func defaultNow() time.Time { return time.Now().UTC().Truncate(time.Microsecond) }
```

- [x] **Step 8: Implement cursors**

`services/order/internal/app/cursor.go`:
```go
package app

import (
	"encoding/base64"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
)

// EncodeCursor renders a keyset position as an opaque URL-safe string.
func EncodeCursor(c Cursor) string {
	raw := strconv.FormatInt(c.CreatedAt.UnixMicro(), 10) + "|" + c.ID
	return base64.RawURLEncoding.EncodeToString([]byte(raw))
}

// DecodeCursor parses a string made by EncodeCursor; anything else is
// ErrInvalidCursor.
func DecodeCursor(s string) (Cursor, error) {
	b, err := base64.RawURLEncoding.DecodeString(s)
	if err != nil {
		return Cursor{}, ErrInvalidCursor
	}
	ts, id, ok := strings.Cut(string(b), "|")
	if !ok {
		return Cursor{}, ErrInvalidCursor
	}
	micros, err := strconv.ParseInt(ts, 10, 64)
	if err != nil {
		return Cursor{}, ErrInvalidCursor
	}
	u, err := uuid.Parse(id)
	if err != nil {
		return Cursor{}, ErrInvalidCursor
	}
	return Cursor{CreatedAt: time.UnixMicro(micros).UTC(), ID: u.String()}, nil
}
```

- [x] **Step 9: Implement OrderService**

`services/order/internal/app/orders.go`:
```go
package app

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"time"

	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/domain"
)

// OrderService reads orders and runs buyer/seller lifecycle actions (WS3 §5).
type OrderService struct {
	store Store
	now   func() time.Time
}

// NewOrderService uses now as its clock (nil = UTC wall clock).
func NewOrderService(store Store, now func() time.Time) *OrderService {
	if now == nil {
		now = defaultNow
	}
	return &OrderService{store: store, now: now}
}

// Get returns an order visible to userID (its buyer or seller), else ErrNotFound.
func (s *OrderService) Get(ctx context.Context, userID, orderID string) (domain.Order, error) {
	o, err := s.store.GetOrder(ctx, orderID)
	if err != nil {
		return domain.Order{}, err
	}
	if o.BuyerID != userID && o.SellerUserID != userID {
		return domain.Order{}, ErrNotFound
	}
	return o, nil
}

// ListInput selects a page of the caller's orders. Limit must be ≥ 1.
type ListInput struct {
	Party  Party
	UserID string
	Status *domain.Status
	Limit  int
	Cursor string // opaque, from a previous Page.NextCursor
}

// Page is one page of orders, newest first.
type Page struct {
	Orders     []domain.Order
	NextCursor *string // nil on the last page
}

func (s *OrderService) List(ctx context.Context, in ListInput) (Page, error) {
	q := ListQuery{Party: in.Party, UserID: in.UserID, Status: in.Status, Limit: in.Limit + 1}
	if in.Cursor != "" {
		c, err := DecodeCursor(in.Cursor)
		if err != nil {
			return Page{}, err
		}
		q.After = &c
	}
	orders, err := s.store.ListOrders(ctx, q)
	if err != nil {
		return Page{}, err
	}
	page := Page{Orders: orders}
	if len(orders) > in.Limit {
		page.Orders = orders[:in.Limit]
		last := page.Orders[in.Limit-1]
		next := EncodeCursor(Cursor{CreatedAt: last.CreatedAt, ID: last.ID})
		page.NextCursor = &next
	}
	return page, nil
}

// ActInput is a buyer or seller action on an order.
type ActInput struct {
	OrderID string
	Actor   domain.Actor
	Action  domain.Action
	Note    string // cancel reason, or optional seller note
}

// Act performs the action. The actor must be the order's buyer (buyer role) or
// seller (seller role), otherwise ErrNotFound. Illegal moves return
// domain.ErrInvalidTransition. A lost optimistic-lock race is retried once
// against the fresh state; losing again returns ErrConcurrentUpdate.
func (s *OrderService) Act(ctx context.Context, in ActInput) (domain.Order, error) {
	for range 2 {
		o, err := s.store.GetOrder(ctx, in.OrderID)
		if err != nil {
			return domain.Order{}, err
		}
		if !canAct(o, in.Actor) {
			return domain.Order{}, ErrNotFound
		}
		err = s.store.InTx(ctx, func(tx Tx) error {
			return applyTransition(ctx, tx, &o, in.Action, in.Actor, in.Note, s.now())
		})
		if errors.Is(err, ErrConcurrentUpdate) {
			continue
		}
		if err != nil {
			return domain.Order{}, err
		}
		return o, nil
	}
	return domain.Order{}, ErrConcurrentUpdate
}

func canAct(o domain.Order, a domain.Actor) bool {
	switch a.Role {
	case domain.RoleBuyer:
		return o.BuyerID == a.ID
	case domain.RoleSeller:
		return o.SellerUserID == a.ID
	default:
		return false
	}
}
```

```go
package app_test

import (
	"fmt"
	"testing"

	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"
	"google.golang.org/grpc/codes"

	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/adapter/catalog/catalogfake"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/app"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/domain"
)

func TestCartService(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t, envOpts{redis: true})
	svc := app.NewCartService(e.carts, e.catalog, nil)

	t.Run("empty cart does not call catalog", func(t *testing.T) {
		before := e.fake.Calls(catalogfake.GetSkus)
		cart, err := svc.Get(ctx, uid())
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.Equal(t, domain.Cart{}, cart)
		assert.Equal(t, before, e.fake.Calls(catalogfake.GetSkus))
	})

	t.Run("set, update and remove items grouped by shop", func(t *testing.T) {
		buyer, shop := uid(), catalogfake.NewShop("Shop Cart")
		a := e.fake.AddSku(shop, 100_000, 3)
		b := e.fake.AddSku(shop, 50_000, 10)

		cart, err := svc.SetItem(ctx, buyer, a.GetSkuId(), 5)
		require.NoError(t, err)
		require.Len(t, cart.Shops, 1)
		assert.Equal(t, int32(5), cart.Shops[0].Items[0].Quantity)
		assert.Equal(t, int32(3), cart.Shops[0].Items[0].Stock, "more than stock is allowed and visible")
		assert.Equal(t, int64(500_000), cart.Subtotal)

		_, err = svc.SetItem(ctx, buyer, b.GetSkuId(), 1)
		require.NoError(t, err)
		cart, err = svc.SetItem(ctx, buyer, a.GetSkuId(), 2)
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.Equal(t, int64(250_000), cart.Subtotal)

		cart, err = svc.RemoveItem(ctx, buyer, b.GetSkuId())
		require.NoError(t, err)
		require.Len(t, cart.Shops[0].Items, 1)
		assert.Equal(t, int64(200_000), cart.Subtotal)
	})

	t.Run("rejects unknown, unavailable, own-shop SKUs and bad quantities", func(t *testing.T) {
		buyer := uid()
		_, err := svc.SetItem(ctx, buyer, uid(), 1)
		assert.ErrorIs(t, err, app.ErrSkuNotFound)

		off := e.fake.AddSku(catalogfake.NewShop("Off"), 10_000, 5)
		e.fake.SetAvailable(off.GetSkuId(), false)
		_, err = svc.SetItem(ctx, buyer, off.GetSkuId(), 1)
		assert.ErrorIs(t, err, app.ErrSkuUnavailable)

		mine := catalogfake.NewShop("Mine")
		mine.SellerUserID = buyer
		own := e.fake.AddSku(mine, 10_000, 5)
		_, err = svc.SetItem(ctx, buyer, own.GetSkuId(), 1)
		assert.ErrorIs(t, err, app.ErrOwnShop)

		ok := e.fake.AddSku(catalogfake.NewShop("Ok"), 10_000, 5)
		for _, q := range []int32{0, 100, -1} {
			_, err = svc.SetItem(ctx, buyer, ok.GetSkuId(), q)
			assert.ErrorIs(t, err, app.ErrInvalidQuantity, fmt.Sprint(q))
		}
		lines, err := e.carts.Lines(ctx, buyer)
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.Empty(t, lines, "nothing was stored")
	})

	t.Run("a SKU that became unavailable is flagged and excluded from subtotal", func(t *testing.T) {
		buyer := uid()
		s := e.fake.AddSku(catalogfake.NewShop("Flag"), 10_000, 5)
		_, err := svc.SetItem(ctx, buyer, s.GetSkuId(), 2)
		require.NoError(t, err)
		e.fake.SetAvailable(s.GetSkuId(), false)

		cart, err := svc.Get(ctx, buyer)
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.False(t, cart.Shops[0].Items[0].Available)
		assert.Zero(t, cart.Subtotal)
	})

	t.Run("SKUs unknown to catalog are omitted and pruned", func(t *testing.T) {
		buyer, ghost := uid(), uid()
		s := e.fake.AddSku(catalogfake.NewShop("Prune"), 10_000, 5)
		e.cartSet(t, buyer, s.GetSkuId(), 1)
		e.cartSet(t, buyer, ghost, 1)

		cart, err := svc.Get(ctx, buyer)
		require.NoError(t, err)
		require.Len(t, cart.Shops, 1)
		assert.Len(t, cart.Shops[0].Items, 1)
		lines, err := e.carts.Lines(ctx, buyer)
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.NotContains(t, lines, ghost)
	})

	t.Run("catalog down is ErrCatalogUnavailable", func(t *testing.T) {
		buyer := uid()
		e.cartSet(t, buyer, uid(), 1)
		e.fake.FailNext(catalogfake.GetSkus, codes.Unavailable, 3)
		_, err := svc.Get(ctx, buyer)
		assert.ErrorIs(t, err, app.ErrCatalogUnavailable)
	})

	t.Run("the 51st distinct line is rejected", func(t *testing.T) {
		buyer, shop := uid(), catalogfake.NewShop("Full")
		for range domain.MaxCartLines {
			e.cartSet(t, buyer, e.fake.AddSku(shop, 1_000, 5).GetSkuId(), 1)
		}
		_, err := svc.SetItem(ctx, buyer, e.fake.AddSku(shop, 1_000, 5).GetSkuId(), 1)
		assert.ErrorIs(t, err, app.ErrCartFull)
	})
}
```

- [x] **Step 3: Implement CartService**

`services/order/internal/app/cart.go`:
```go
package app

import (
	"context"
	"log/slog"
	"maps"
	"slices"

	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/domain"
)

// CartService manages the buyer's cart (WS3 §3).
type CartService struct {
	carts   CartStore
	catalog Catalog
	logger  *slog.Logger
}

// NewCartService uses logger for best-effort failures (nil = slog.Default()).
func NewCartService(carts CartStore, catalog Catalog, logger *slog.Logger) *CartService {
	if logger == nil {
		logger = slog.Default()
	}
	return &CartService{carts: carts, catalog: catalog, logger: logger}
}

// Get returns the cart enriched with current catalog data. Lines whose SKU
// catalog no longer knows are left out and pruned from the store (best effort).
func (s *CartService) Get(ctx context.Context, userID string) (domain.Cart, error) {
	lines, err := s.carts.Lines(ctx, userID)
	if err != nil {
		return domain.Cart{}, err
	}
	if len(lines) == 0 {
		return domain.Cart{}, nil
	}
	skus, err := s.catalog.GetSkus(ctx, slices.Sorted(maps.Keys(lines)))
	if err != nil {
		return domain.Cart{}, err
	}
	cart, missing := domain.BuildCart(lines, skus)
	if len(missing) > 0 {
		if err := s.carts.Remove(ctx, userID, missing...); err != nil {
			s.logger.WarnContext(ctx, "prune unknown cart skus failed", "user_id", userID, "error", err)
		}
	}
	return cart, nil
}

// SetItem sets the quantity of a line, creating it if needed. The SKU must
// exist, be available and not belong to the caller's own shop; a quantity
// above current stock is allowed (checkout enforces stock).
func (s *CartService) SetItem(ctx context.Context, userID, skuID string, quantity int32) (domain.Cart, error) {
	if quantity < domain.MinQuantity || quantity > domain.MaxQuantity {
		return domain.Cart{}, ErrInvalidQuantity
	}
	skus, err := s.catalog.GetSkus(ctx, []string{skuID})
	if err != nil {
		return domain.Cart{}, err
	}
	sku, ok := skus[skuID]
	switch {
	case !ok:
		return domain.Cart{}, ErrSkuNotFound
	case sku.SellerUserID == userID:
		return domain.Cart{}, ErrOwnShop
	case !sku.Available:
		return domain.Cart{}, ErrSkuUnavailable
	}
	if err := s.carts.Set(ctx, userID, skuID, quantity); err != nil {
		return domain.Cart{}, err
	}
	return s.Get(ctx, userID)
}

// RemoveItem deletes a line (a no-op if absent) and returns the cart.
func (s *CartService) RemoveItem(ctx context.Context, userID, skuID string) (domain.Cart, error) {
	if err := s.carts.Remove(ctx, userID, skuID); err != nil {
		return domain.Cart{}, err
	}
	return s.Get(ctx, userID)
}
```

```go
package app_test

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"net/http"
	"sync"
	"testing"

	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"
	"google.golang.org/grpc/codes"

	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/pkg/event"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/adapter/catalog/catalogfake"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/app"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/domain"
)

// recorder is an app.Encoder that remembers what it was asked to encode.
// Errors render as 409 here; real status mapping is adapter/http's job.
type recorder struct {
	mu  sync.Mutex
	err error
}

func (r *recorder) encode(res app.CheckoutResult, err error) app.Response {
	r.mu.Lock()
	defer r.mu.Unlock()
	r.err = err
	if err != nil {
		b, _ := json.Marshal(map[string]string{"error": err.Error()})
		return app.Response{Status: http.StatusConflict, Body: b}
	}
	ids := make([]string, len(res.Orders))
	for i, o := range res.Orders {
		ids[i] = o.ID
	}
	b, _ := json.Marshal(map[string]any{"checkout_id": res.CheckoutID, "order_ids": ids})
	return app.Response{Status: http.StatusCreated, Body: b}
}

func (r *recorder) lastErr() error {
	r.mu.Lock()
	defer r.mu.Unlock()
	return r.err
}

func checkoutInput(buyer, key string, skuIDs ...string) app.CheckoutInput {
	return app.CheckoutInput{BuyerID: buyer, IdempotencyKey: key, SkuIDs: skuIDs, Address: testAddress, PaymentMethod: domain.PaymentCOD}
}

func createdOrderIDs(t *testing.T, resp app.Response) []string {
	t.Helper()
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusCreated, resp.Status, string(resp.Body))
	var body struct {
		OrderIDs []string `json:"order_ids"`
	}
	require.NoError(t, json.Unmarshal(resp.Body, &body))
	return body.OrderIDs
}

func buyerOrders(t *testing.T, e *env, buyer string) []domain.Order {
	t.Helper()
	orders, err := e.store.ListOrders(ctx, app.ListQuery{Party: app.PartyBuyer, UserID: buyer, Limit: 100})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	return orders
}

func TestRequestHashIgnoresSkuOrderAndDuplicates(t *testing.T) {
	a := app.RequestHash(checkoutInput("b", "k", "s2", "s1"))
	assert.Equal(t, a, app.RequestHash(checkoutInput("b", "k", "s1", "s2", "s1")))
	assert.NotEqual(t, a, app.RequestHash(checkoutInput("b", "k", "s1")))
	assert.NotEqual(t, app.RequestHash(checkoutInput("b", "k")), app.RequestHash(checkoutInput("b", "k", "s1")), "whole cart differs from a selection")
}

func TestCheckout(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t, envOpts{postgres: true, redis: true})
	svc := app.NewCheckoutService(e.store, e.carts, e.catalog, app.CheckoutConfig{ShippingFee: 30_000, Now: e.clock.Now})

	t.Run("two shops become two orders pending confirmation", func(t *testing.T) {
		buyer, shopA, shopB := uid(), catalogfake.NewShop("A"), catalogfake.NewShop("B")
		a := e.fake.AddSku(shopA, 100_000, 10)
		b := e.fake.AddSku(shopB, 40_000, 5)
		e.cartSet(t, buyer, a.GetSkuId(), 2)
		e.cartSet(t, buyer, b.GetSkuId(), 1)
		reserves := e.fake.Calls(catalogfake.ReserveStock)

		rec := &recorder{}
		resp, err := svc.Checkout(ctx, checkoutInput(buyer, "k1"), rec.encode)
		require.NoError(t, err)
		ids := createdOrderIDs(t, resp)
		require.Len(t, ids, 2)

		assert.Equal(t, reserves+2, e.fake.Calls(catalogfake.ReserveStock))
		assert.Equal(t, int32(8), e.fake.Stock(a.GetSkuId()))
		assert.Equal(t, int32(4), e.fake.Stock(b.GetSkuId()))
		totals := map[string]int64{}
		checkoutIDs := map[string]bool{}
		for _, id := range ids {
			o, err := e.store.GetOrder(ctx, id)
			require.NoError(t, err)
			assert.Equal(t, domain.StatusPendingConfirmation, o.Status)
			assert.Equal(t, int64(30_000), o.ShippingFee)
			assert.Len(t, o.History, 2)
			assert.True(t, e.fake.Reserved(o.ID))
			assert.Equal(t, []event.Type{event.TypeOrderCreated, event.TypeOrderStatusChanged}, types(e.events(t, id)))
			totals[o.ShopID] = o.Total
			checkoutIDs[o.CheckoutID] = true
		}
		assert.Equal(t, map[string]int64{shopA.ID: 230_000, shopB.ID: 70_000}, totals)
		assert.Len(t, checkoutIDs, 1, "one checkout_id for the whole checkout")

		lines, err := e.carts.Lines(ctx, buyer)
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.Empty(t, lines, "purchased lines are removed from the cart")
	})

	t.Run("selected sku_ids only; equivalent retries replay without new reservations", func(t *testing.T) {
		buyer, shop := uid(), catalogfake.NewShop("Sel")
		a := e.fake.AddSku(shop, 10_000, 10)
		b := e.fake.AddSku(shop, 20_000, 10)
		c := e.fake.AddSku(catalogfake.NewShop("Other"), 5_000, 10)
		for _, s := range []string{a.GetSkuId(), b.GetSkuId(), c.GetSkuId()} {
			e.cartSet(t, buyer, s, 1)
		}
		rec := &recorder{}
		first, err := svc.Checkout(ctx, checkoutInput(buyer, "sel", b.GetSkuId(), a.GetSkuId()), rec.encode)
		require.NoError(t, err)
		require.Len(t, createdOrderIDs(t, first), 1)
		reserves := e.fake.Calls(catalogfake.ReserveStock)

		again, err := svc.Checkout(ctx, checkoutInput(buyer, "sel", a.GetSkuId(), b.GetSkuId(), a.GetSkuId()), rec.encode)
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.Equal(t, first.Status, again.Status)
		assert.JSONEq(t, string(first.Body), string(again.Body))
		assert.Equal(t, reserves, e.fake.Calls(catalogfake.ReserveStock))

		lines, err := e.carts.Lines(ctx, buyer)
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.Equal(t, map[string]int32{c.GetSkuId(): 1}, lines)
	})

	t.Run("same key with a different body is rejected", func(t *testing.T) {
		buyer := uid()
		s := e.fake.AddSku(catalogfake.NewShop("Reuse"), 10_000, 10)
		e.cartSet(t, buyer, s.GetSkuId(), 1)
		rec := &recorder{}
		_, err := svc.Checkout(ctx, checkoutInput(buyer, "reuse"), rec.encode)
		require.NoError(t, err)

		other := checkoutInput(buyer, "reuse")
		other.Address.Line1 = "2 Hai Bà Trưng"

	t.Run("same key still in progress is a conflict", func(t *testing.T) {
		buyer := uid()

		_, created, err := e.store.BeginIdempotency(ctx, buyer, "busy", app.RequestHash(in), e.clock.Now())
		require.NoError(t, err)
		require.True(t, created)

	})

	t.Run("empty cart is a stored rejection without catalog calls", func(t *testing.T) {
		buyer, rec := uid(), &recorder{}
		calls := e.fake.Calls(catalogfake.GetSkus)
		resp, err := svc.Checkout(ctx, checkoutInput(buyer, "empty"), rec.encode)
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.ErrorIs(t, rec.lastErr(), app.ErrEmptyCart)
		replay, err := svc.Checkout(ctx, checkoutInput(buyer, "empty"), rec.encode)
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.JSONEq(t, string(resp.Body), string(replay.Body))
		assert.Equal(t, calls, e.fake.Calls(catalogfake.GetSkus))
	})

	t.Run("sku_ids not in the cart", func(t *testing.T) {
		buyer, missing, rec := uid(), uid(), &recorder{}
		s := e.fake.AddSku(catalogfake.NewShop("Miss"), 10_000, 10)
		e.cartSet(t, buyer, s.GetSkuId(), 1)
		_, err := svc.Checkout(ctx, checkoutInput(buyer, "miss", s.GetSkuId(), missing), rec.encode)
		require.NoError(t, err)
		var sel *app.SelectionError
		require.ErrorAs(t, rec.lastErr(), &sel)
		assert.Equal(t, []string{missing}, sel.NotInCart)
	})

	t.Run("unavailable and own-shop lines create nothing", func(t *testing.T) {
		buyer, rec := uid(), &recorder{}
		mine := catalogfake.NewShop("Mine")
		mine.SellerUserID = buyer
		own := e.fake.AddSku(mine, 10_000, 10)
		off := e.fake.AddSku(catalogfake.NewShop("Off"), 10_000, 10)
		e.fake.SetAvailable(off.GetSkuId(), false)
		ok := e.fake.AddSku(catalogfake.NewShop("Ok"), 10_000, 10)
		for _, s := range []string{own.GetSkuId(), off.GetSkuId(), ok.GetSkuId()} {
			e.cartSet(t, buyer, s, 1)
		}
		reserves := e.fake.Calls(catalogfake.ReserveStock)

		_, err := svc.Checkout(ctx, checkoutInput(buyer, "bad-lines"), rec.encode)
		require.NoError(t, err)
		var lp *domain.LineProblemsError
		require.ErrorAs(t, rec.lastErr(), &lp)
		assert.ElementsMatch(t, []domain.LineProblem{
			{SkuID: own.GetSkuId(), Reason: domain.ProblemOwnShop},
			{SkuID: off.GetSkuId(), Reason: domain.ProblemUnavailable},
		}, lp.Problems)
		assert.Empty(t, buyerOrders(t, e, buyer))
		assert.Equal(t, reserves, e.fake.Calls(catalogfake.ReserveStock))
		lines, err := e.carts.Lines(ctx, buyer)
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.Len(t, lines, 3)
	})

	t.Run("catalog unavailable is not stored so the same key can be retried", func(t *testing.T) {
		buyer := uid()
		s := e.fake.AddSku(catalogfake.NewShop("Down"), 10_000, 10)
		e.cartSet(t, buyer, s.GetSkuId(), 1)
		e.fake.FailNext(catalogfake.GetSkus, codes.Unavailable, 3)

		_, err := svc.Checkout(ctx, checkoutInput(buyer, "down"), (&recorder{}).encode)
		require.ErrorIs(t, err, app.ErrCatalogUnavailable)
		resp, err := svc.Checkout(ctx, checkoutInput(buyer, "down"), (&recorder{}).encode)
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.Len(t, createdOrderIDs(t, resp), 1)
	})

	t.Run("a cancelled request context still completes the checkout", func(t *testing.T) {
		buyer := uid()
		s := e.fake.AddSku(catalogfake.NewShop("Gone"), 10_000, 10)
		e.cartSet(t, buyer, s.GetSkuId(), 1)
		cctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(ctx)
		cancel()

		resp, err := svc.Checkout(cctx, checkoutInput(buyer, "gone"), (&recorder{}).encode)
		require.NoError(t, err)
		ids := createdOrderIDs(t, resp)
		o, err := e.store.GetOrder(ctx, ids[0])
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.Equal(t, domain.StatusPendingConfirmation, o.Status)
	})
}
```

- [x] **Step 3: Implement CheckoutService (without compensation yet)**

`services/order/internal/app/checkout.go`:
```go
package app

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"log/slog"
	"maps"
	"slices"
	"strings"
	"sync"
	"time"

	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/domain"
)

// CheckoutConfig configures CheckoutService.
type CheckoutConfig struct {
	ShippingFee int64            // flat fee per order (SHIPPING_FEE_FLAT)
	Now         func() time.Time // nil = UTC wall clock
	Logger      *slog.Logger     // nil = slog.Default()
}

// CheckoutInput is a validated checkout request.
type CheckoutInput struct {
	BuyerID        string
	IdempotencyKey string
	SkuIDs         []string // nil = the whole cart
	Address        domain.Address
	PaymentMethod  string
}

// CheckoutResult is a successful checkout.
type CheckoutResult struct {
	CheckoutID string
	Orders     []domain.Order
}

// Encoder renders a checkout outcome — a result, or a business error — as the
// response that is stored for idempotent replays and sent to the client.
type Encoder func(res CheckoutResult, err error) Response

// CheckoutService turns a cart into one order per shop (WS3 §4).
type CheckoutService struct {
	store   Store
	carts   CartStore
	catalog Catalog
	cfg     CheckoutConfig
}

func NewCheckoutService(store Store, carts CartStore, catalog Catalog, cfg CheckoutConfig) *CheckoutService {
	if cfg.Now == nil {
		cfg.Now = defaultNow
	}
	if cfg.Logger == nil {
		cfg.Logger = slog.Default()
	}
	return &CheckoutService{store: store, carts: carts, catalog: catalog, cfg: cfg}
}

// RequestHash fingerprints a checkout request for idempotency. Equivalent
// requests (same SKUs in any order, duplicates ignored) hash the same.
func RequestHash(in CheckoutInput) []byte {
	b, _ := json.Marshal(struct { // strings only: cannot fail
		SkuIDs        []string       `json:"sku_ids"`
		Address       domain.Address `json:"shipping_address"`
		PaymentMethod string         `json:"payment_method"`
	}{normalizeIDs(in.SkuIDs), in.Address, in.PaymentMethod})
	sum := sha256.Sum256(b)
	return sum[:]
}

// Checkout runs a checkout idempotently per (buyer, key):
//   - same key and request, completed → the stored response;
//   - same key and request, still running → ErrIdempotencyInProgress;
//   - same key, different request → ErrIdempotencyKeyReused.
//
// Successes and deterministic rejections are rendered by enc, stored and
// returned as a Response. Transient failures are returned as errors and free
// the key so the client can retry with it. Cancellation of ctx is ignored:
// once orders exist the checkout must finish or compensate.
func (s *CheckoutService) Checkout(ctx context.Context, in CheckoutInput, enc Encoder) (Response, error) {
	ctx = context.WithoutCancel(ctx)
	in.SkuIDs = normalizeIDs(in.SkuIDs)
	hash := RequestHash(in)
	rec, created, err := s.store.BeginIdempotency(ctx, in.BuyerID, in.IdempotencyKey, hash, s.cfg.Now())
	if err != nil {
		return Response{}, err
	}
	if !created {
		switch {
		case !bytes.Equal(rec.RequestHash, hash):
			return Response{}, ErrIdempotencyKeyReused
		case rec.Response == nil:
			return Response{}, ErrIdempotencyInProgress
		default:
			return *rec.Response, nil
		}
	}
	resp, err := s.run(ctx, in, enc)
	if err != nil {
		if derr := s.store.DeleteIdempotencyKey(ctx, in.BuyerID, in.IdempotencyKey); derr != nil {
			s.cfg.Logger.ErrorContext(ctx, "release idempotency key failed", "buyer_id", in.BuyerID, "error", derr)
		}
		return Response{}, err
	}
	return resp, nil
}

func (s *CheckoutService) run(ctx context.Context, in CheckoutInput, enc Encoder) (Response, error) {
	lines, err := s.selectLines(ctx, in)
	if err != nil {
		return s.reject(ctx, in, enc, err)
	}
	skus, err := s.catalog.GetSkus(ctx, skuIDsOf(lines))
	if err != nil {
		return Response{}, err
	}
	checkoutID := newID()
	orders, err := domain.BuildOrders(domain.CheckoutParams{
		CheckoutID: checkoutID, BuyerID: in.BuyerID, Lines: lines, Skus: skus,
		ShippingFee: s.cfg.ShippingFee, Address: in.Address, PaymentMethod: in.PaymentMethod,
		Now: s.cfg.Now(), NewID: newID,
	})
	if err != nil {
		return s.reject(ctx, in, enc, err)
	}
	// Tx A: the orders exist (PENDING) before any stock is reserved, so the
	// sweeper can always find and compensate a checkout that dies midway.
	err = s.store.InTx(ctx, func(tx Tx) error {
		for _, o := range orders {
			if err := tx.InsertOrder(ctx, o); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
		return nil
	})
	if err != nil {
		return Response{}, err
	}
	if err := s.reserveAll(ctx, orders); err != nil {
		return Response{}, err // compensation: Task 11
	}
	return s.commit(ctx, in, enc, CheckoutResult{CheckoutID: checkoutID, Orders: orders}, lines)
}

// selectLines returns the cart lines to buy, sorted by SKU ID.
func (s *CheckoutService) selectLines(ctx context.Context, in CheckoutInput) ([]domain.Line, error) {
	cart, err := s.carts.Lines(ctx, in.BuyerID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	ids := in.SkuIDs
	if ids == nil {
		ids = slices.Sorted(maps.Keys(cart))
	} else {
		var missing []string
		for _, id := range ids {
			if _, ok := cart[id]; !ok {
				missing = append(missing, id)
			}
		}
		if len(missing) > 0 {
			return nil, &SelectionError{NotInCart: missing}
		}
	}
	if len(ids) == 0 {
		return nil, ErrEmptyCart
	}
	lines := make([]domain.Line, len(ids))
	for i, id := range ids {
		lines[i] = domain.Line{SkuID: id, Quantity: cart[id]}
	}
	return lines, nil
}

// reject ends a checkout that failed before any order existed.
func (s *CheckoutService) reject(ctx context.Context, in CheckoutInput, enc Encoder, cause error) (Response, error) {
	if !storable(cause) {
		return Response{}, cause
	}
	resp := enc(CheckoutResult{}, cause)
	err := s.store.InTx(ctx, func(tx Tx) error {
		return tx.CompleteIdempotencyKey(ctx, in.BuyerID, in.IdempotencyKey, resp)
	})
	if err != nil {
		return Response{}, err
	}
	return resp, nil
}

// reserveAll reserves stock for every order concurrently; nil only if all
// succeeded.
func (s *CheckoutService) reserveAll(ctx context.Context, orders []domain.Order) error {
	errs := make([]error, len(orders))
	var wg sync.WaitGroup
	for i, o := range orders {
		wg.Add(1)
		go func() {
			defer wg.Done()
			errs[i] = s.catalog.ReserveStock(ctx, o.ID, o.StockLines())
		}()
	}
	wg.Wait()
	return mergeReserveErrors(errs)
}

// mergeReserveErrors prefers OUT_OF_STOCK (with every shop's shortages) over
// other failures.
func mergeReserveErrors(errs []error) error {
	var merged *OutOfStockError
	var first error
	for _, err := range errs {
		var oos *OutOfStockError
		switch {
		case err == nil:
		case errors.As(err, &oos):
			if merged == nil {
				merged = &OutOfStockError{}
			}
			merged.Shortages = append(merged.Shortages, oos.Shortages...)
		case first == nil:
			first = err
		}
	}
	if merged != nil {
		slices.SortFunc(merged.Shortages, func(a, b domain.Shortage) int { return strings.Compare(a.SkuID, b.SkuID) })
		return merged
	}
	return first
}

// commit is Tx C: every order moves to PENDING_CONFIRMATION with its events
// and the response is stored, atomically.
func (s *CheckoutService) commit(ctx context.Context, in CheckoutInput, enc Encoder, res CheckoutResult, lines []domain.Line) (Response, error) {
	now := s.cfg.Now()
	var resp Response
	err := s.store.InTx(ctx, func(tx Tx) error {
		for i := range res.Orders {
			o := &res.Orders[i]
			if err := tx.Publish(ctx, o.ID, orderCreated(*o), now); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			if err := applyTransition(ctx, tx, o, domain.ActionSubmit, domain.SystemActor(), "", now); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
		resp = enc(res, nil)
		return tx.CompleteIdempotencyKey(ctx, in.BuyerID, in.IdempotencyKey, resp)
	})
	if errors.Is(err, ErrConcurrentUpdate) {
		// The sweeper cancelled these orders first (the checkout outlived
		// PENDING_TIMEOUT): give back whatever we reserved after it released.
		s.releaseAll(ctx, res.Orders)
		return Response{}, ErrCheckoutExpired
	}
	if err != nil {
		return Response{}, err
	}
	if err := s.carts.Remove(ctx, in.BuyerID, skuIDsOf(lines)...); err != nil {
		s.cfg.Logger.WarnContext(ctx, "clear purchased cart lines failed", "buyer_id", in.BuyerID, "error", err)
	}
	return resp, nil
}

// releaseAll releases every order's stock, best effort.
func (s *CheckoutService) releaseAll(ctx context.Context, orders []domain.Order) {
	for _, o := range orders {
		if err := s.catalog.ReleaseStock(ctx, o.ID); err != nil {
			s.cfg.Logger.WarnContext(ctx, "release stock failed", "order_id", o.ID, "error", err)
		}
	}
}

// storable reports whether a failure is a final answer for this request
// (stored and replayed) rather than a transient one.
func storable(err error) bool {
	var (
		sel *SelectionError
		lp  *domain.LineProblemsError
		oos *OutOfStockError
	)
	return errors.Is(err, ErrEmptyCart) || errors.As(err, &sel) || errors.As(err, &lp) ||
		errors.As(err, &oos) || errors.Is(err, ErrCatalogRejected)
}

func normalizeIDs(ids []string) []string {
	if ids == nil {
		return nil
	}
	out := slices.Clone(ids)
	slices.Sort(out)
	return slices.Compact(out)
}

func skuIDsOf(lines []domain.Line) []string {
	ids := make([]string, len(lines))
	for i, l := range lines {
		ids[i] = l.SkuID
	}
	return ids
}
```

```go
		require.NoError(t, err)

	})
}
```

```go
// abort compensates a failed reservation (WS3 §4 step 7): release every order
// of the checkout, then Tx B cancels them all (system, OUT_OF_STOCK or
// RESERVE_FAILED). Release failures are only logged: the OrderCancelled events
// written in Tx B make catalog release the stock asynchronously.
func (s *CheckoutService) abort(ctx context.Context, in CheckoutInput, enc Encoder, orders []domain.Order, cause error) (Response, error) {
	s.releaseAll(ctx, orders)
	reason := domain.CancelReserveFailed
	var oos *OutOfStockError
	if errors.As(cause, &oos) {
		reason = domain.CancelOutOfStock
	}
	store := storable(cause)
	now := s.cfg.Now()
	var resp Response
	err := s.store.InTx(ctx, func(tx Tx) error {
		for i := range orders {
			if err := applyTransition(ctx, tx, &orders[i], domain.ActionCancel, domain.SystemActor(), reason, now); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
		if !store {
			return nil
		}
		resp = enc(CheckoutResult{}, cause)
		return tx.CompleteIdempotencyKey(ctx, in.BuyerID, in.IdempotencyKey, resp)
	})
	if err != nil {
		return Response{}, fmt.Errorf("cancel orders after failed reservation: %w", err)
	}
	if !store {
		return Response{}, cause
	}
	return resp, nil
}
```

```go
package app_test

import (
	"context"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"
	"google.golang.org/grpc/codes"

	catalogv1 "github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/gen/catalog/v1"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/pkg/event"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/adapter/catalog/catalogfake"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/app"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/domain"
)

// crashedCheckout stores a PENDING order created at `at` holding a stock
// reservation, as if the service died between Tx A and Tx C.
func crashedCheckout(t *testing.T, e *env, at time.Time, stock int32) (domain.Order, *catalogv1.SkuInfo) {
	t.Helper()
	s := e.fake.AddSku(catalogfake.NewShop("Crash"), 10_000, stock)
	skus, err := e.catalog.GetSkus(ctx, []string{s.GetSkuId()})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	orders, err := domain.BuildOrders(domain.CheckoutParams{
		CheckoutID: uid(), BuyerID: uid(), Lines: []domain.Line{{SkuID: s.GetSkuId(), Quantity: 2}}, Skus: skus,
		ShippingFee: 30_000, Address: testAddress, PaymentMethod: domain.PaymentCOD, Now: at, NewID: uid,
	})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	o := orders[0]
	require.NoError(t, e.store.InTx(ctx, func(tx app.Tx) error { return tx.InsertOrder(ctx, o) }))
	require.NoError(t, e.catalog.ReserveStock(ctx, o.ID, o.StockLines()))
	return o, s
}

// sweepDuringReserve runs `run` once, just before the first reservation.
type sweepDuringReserve struct {
	app.Catalog
	once sync.Once
	run  func()
}

func (c *sweepDuringReserve) ReserveStock(ctx context.Context, orderID string, lines []domain.Line) error {
	c.once.Do(c.run)
	return c.Catalog.ReserveStock(ctx, orderID, lines)
}

func TestSweeper(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t, envOpts{postgres: true, redis: true})
	sw := app.NewSweeper(e.store, e.catalog, app.SweeperConfig{
		Interval: time.Minute, PendingTimeout: 2 * time.Minute, BatchSize: 2, Now: e.clock.Now,
	})

	t.Run("stale pending orders are cancelled and released", func(t *testing.T) {
		o, s := crashedCheckout(t, e, e.clock.Now(), 5)
		require.Equal(t, int32(3), e.fake.Stock(s.GetSkuId()))
		e.clock.Advance(3 * time.Minute)

		n, err := sw.RunOnce(ctx)
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.Equal(t, 1, n)
		got, err := e.store.GetOrder(ctx, o.ID)
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.Equal(t, domain.StatusCancelled, got.Status)
		assert.Equal(t, domain.CancelCheckoutTimeout, *got.CancelReason)
		assert.Equal(t, domain.RoleSystem, got.History[1].ActorRole)
		assert.Equal(t, int32(5), e.fake.Stock(s.GetSkuId()))
		assert.False(t, e.fake.Reserved(o.ID))

		evs := e.events(t, o.ID)
		require.Equal(t, []event.Type{event.TypeOrderStatusChanged, event.TypeOrderCancelled}, types(evs))
		c, err := event.DecodeData[event.OrderCancelled](evs[1])
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.Equal(t, event.OrderCancelled{OrderID: o.ID, Reason: "CHECKOUT_TIMEOUT", CancelledBy: "system"}, c)
	})

	t.Run("fresh pending and non-pending orders are left alone", func(t *testing.T) {
		old := e.seedOrder(t, uid(), catalogfake.NewShop("Old"), domain.StatusPendingConfirmation)
		e.clock.Advance(3 * time.Minute)
		fresh, _ := crashedCheckout(t, e, e.clock.Now(), 5)
		e.clock.Advance(time.Minute)

		n, err := sw.RunOnce(ctx)
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.Zero(t, n)
		for id, want := range map[string]domain.Status{old.ID: domain.StatusPendingConfirmation, fresh.ID: domain.StatusPending} {
			got, err := e.store.GetOrder(ctx, id)
			require.NoError(t, err)
			assert.Equal(t, want, got.Status)
		}
		e.clock.Advance(2 * time.Minute) // let `fresh` go stale and clean it up
		_, err = sw.RunOnce(ctx)
		require.NoError(t, err)
	})

	t.Run("release failures do not block cancellation", func(t *testing.T) {
		o, _ := crashedCheckout(t, e, e.clock.Now(), 5)
		e.clock.Advance(3 * time.Minute)
		e.fake.FailNext(catalogfake.ReleaseStock, codes.Unavailable, 3)

		_, err := sw.RunOnce(ctx)
		require.NoError(t, err)
		got, err := e.store.GetOrder(ctx, o.ID)
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.Equal(t, domain.StatusCancelled, got.Status)
		assert.True(t, e.fake.Reserved(o.ID), "catalog will release it when it consumes OrderCancelled")
		assert.Contains(t, types(e.events(t, o.ID)), event.TypeOrderCancelled)
	})

	t.Run("works through every batch", func(t *testing.T) {
		for range 5 {
			crashedCheckout(t, e, e.clock.Now(), 5)
		}
		e.clock.Advance(3 * time.Minute)
		n, err := sw.RunOnce(ctx)
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.Equal(t, 5, n, "batch size 2 → 3 batches in one run")
	})

	t.Run("expired idempotency keys are purged", func(t *testing.T) {
		user, hash := uid(), []byte("h")
		_, _, err := e.store.BeginIdempotency(ctx, user, "k", hash, e.clock.Now())
		require.NoError(t, err)
		e.clock.Advance(25 * time.Hour)
		_, err = sw.RunOnce(ctx)
		require.NoError(t, err)
		_, created, err := e.store.BeginIdempotency(ctx, user, "k", hash, e.clock.Now())
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.True(t, created)
	})

	t.Run("a checkout that outlives PENDING_TIMEOUT loses to the sweeper", func(t *testing.T) {
		hook := &sweepDuringReserve{Catalog: e.catalog, run: func() {
			e.clock.Advance(3 * time.Minute)
			_, err := sw.RunOnce(ctx)
			assert.NoError(t, err)
		}}
		svc := app.NewCheckoutService(e.store, e.carts, hook, app.CheckoutConfig{ShippingFee: 30_000, Now: e.clock.Now})
		buyer := uid()
		s := e.fake.AddSku(catalogfake.NewShop("Late"), 10_000, 5)
		e.cartSet(t, buyer, s.GetSkuId(), 1)

		_, err := svc.Checkout(ctx, checkoutInput(buyer, "late"), (&recorder{}).encode)
		require.ErrorIs(t, err, app.ErrCheckoutExpired)
		orders := buyerOrders(t, e, buyer)
		require.Len(t, orders, 1)
		assert.Equal(t, domain.StatusCancelled, orders[0].Status)
		assert.Equal(t, domain.CancelCheckoutTimeout, *orders[0].CancelReason)
		assert.Equal(t, int32(5), e.fake.Stock(s.GetSkuId()))
		assert.False(t, e.fake.Reserved(orders[0].ID))
		lines, err := e.carts.Lines(ctx, buyer)
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.Len(t, lines, 1, "cart kept")
	})
}

// TestSweeperRunBoundsPendingLifetime checks acceptance criterion 2: no order
// stays PENDING longer than PENDING_TIMEOUT + one sweeper interval.
func TestSweeperRunBoundsPendingLifetime(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t, envOpts{postgres: true})
	const timeout, interval = 400 * time.Millisecond, 200 * time.Millisecond
	realNow := func() time.Time { return time.Now().UTC().Truncate(time.Microsecond) }
	sw := app.NewSweeper(e.store, e.catalog, app.SweeperConfig{Interval: interval, PendingTimeout: timeout, Now: realNow})
	o, _ := crashedCheckout(t, e, realNow(), 5)

	rctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(ctx)
	done := make(chan error, 1)
	go func() { done <- sw.Run(rctx) }()
	t.Cleanup(func() {
		cancel()
		assert.NoError(t, <-done)
	})

	var got domain.Order
	require.Eventually(t, func() bool {
		var err error
		got, err = e.store.GetOrder(ctx, o.ID)
		return err == nil && got.Status == domain.StatusCancelled
	}, timeout+interval+2*time.Second, 20*time.Millisecond)
	lifetime := got.History[len(got.History)-1].At.Sub(o.CreatedAt)
	assert.GreaterOrEqual(t, lifetime, timeout)
	assert.LessOrEqual(t, lifetime, timeout+interval+250*time.Millisecond, "250ms slack for scheduling and the transaction")
}
```

- [x] **Step 3: Implement the sweeper**

`services/order/internal/app/sweeper.go`:
```go
package app

import (
	"context"
	"log/slog"
	"time"

	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/domain"
)

// SweeperConfig configures Sweeper. Zero BatchSize / IdempotencyTTL use 100 / 24h.
type SweeperConfig struct {
	Interval       time.Duration // SWEEPER_INTERVAL, > 0
	PendingTimeout time.Duration // PENDING_TIMEOUT
	BatchSize      int
	IdempotencyTTL time.Duration
	Now            func() time.Time
	Logger         *slog.Logger
}

// Sweeper cancels orders stuck in PENDING (a checkout that crashed between
// Tx A and Tx C) and purges expired idempotency keys (WS3 §4). Replicas may
// run it concurrently: stale orders are claimed with FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED.
type Sweeper struct {
	store   Store
	catalog Catalog
	cfg     SweeperConfig
}

func NewSweeper(store Store, catalog Catalog, cfg SweeperConfig) *Sweeper {
	if cfg.BatchSize <= 0 {
		cfg.BatchSize = 100
	}
	if cfg.IdempotencyTTL <= 0 {
		cfg.IdempotencyTTL = 24 * time.Hour
	}
	if cfg.Now == nil {
		cfg.Now = defaultNow
	}
	if cfg.Logger == nil {
		cfg.Logger = slog.Default()
	}
	return &Sweeper{store: store, catalog: catalog, cfg: cfg}
}

// Run sweeps immediately and then every Interval until ctx is cancelled.
func (s *Sweeper) Run(ctx context.Context) error {
	ticker := time.NewTicker(s.cfg.Interval)
	defer ticker.Stop()
	for {
		if _, err := s.RunOnce(ctx); err != nil && ctx.Err() == nil {
			s.cfg.Logger.ErrorContext(ctx, "sweeper run failed", "error", err)
		}
		select {
		case <-ctx.Done():
			return nil
		case <-ticker.C:
		}
	}
}

// RunOnce cancels every stale PENDING order batch by batch (system,
// CHECKOUT_TIMEOUT, with events), then releases their stock outside the row
// locks (best effort), then purges expired idempotency keys. It returns how
// many orders it cancelled.
func (s *Sweeper) RunOnce(ctx context.Context) (int, error) {
	total := 0
	for {
		now := s.cfg.Now()
		var cancelled []domain.Order
		err := s.store.InTx(ctx, func(tx Tx) error {
			orders, err := tx.ClaimStalePending(ctx, now.Add(-s.cfg.PendingTimeout), s.cfg.BatchSize)
			if err != nil {
				return err
			}
			for i := range orders {
				if err := applyTransition(ctx, tx, &orders[i], domain.ActionCancel, domain.SystemActor(), domain.CancelCheckoutTimeout, now); err != nil {
					return err
				}
			}
			cancelled = orders
			return nil
		})
		if err != nil {
			return total, err
		}
		for _, o := range cancelled {
			if err := s.catalog.ReleaseStock(ctx, o.ID); err != nil {
				s.cfg.Logger.WarnContext(ctx, "release stock of timed-out order failed; catalog releases it on OrderCancelled",
					"order_id", o.ID, "error", err)
			}
		}
		total += len(cancelled)
		if len(cancelled) < s.cfg.BatchSize {
			break
		}
	}
	if _, err := s.store.DeleteIdempotencyKeysBefore(ctx, s.cfg.Now().Add(-s.cfg.IdempotencyTTL)); err != nil {
		return total, err
	}
	return total, nil
}
```

```go
func TestToAppError(t *testing.T) {
	fields := func(f map[string]string) map[string]any { return map[string]any{"fields": f} }
	cases := []struct {
		name    string
		err     error
		status  int
		code    string
		details map[string]any
	}{
		{"order not found", app.ErrNotFound, 404, httpx.CodeNotFound, nil},
		{"sku not found", fmt.Errorf("wrapped: %w", app.ErrSkuNotFound), 404, httpx.CodeNotFound, nil},
		{"sku unavailable", app.ErrSkuUnavailable, 409, httpx.CodeConflict, nil},
		{"own shop", app.ErrOwnShop, 409, httpx.CodeConflict, nil},
		{"cart full", app.ErrCartFull, 409, httpx.CodeConflict, nil},
		{"quantity", app.ErrInvalidQuantity, 400, httpx.CodeValidation, fields(map[string]string{"quantity": "must be between 1 and 99"})},
		{"empty cart", app.ErrEmptyCart, 400, httpx.CodeValidation, fields(map[string]string{"sku_ids": "no cart items to check out"})},
		{"selection", &app.SelectionError{NotInCart: []string{"s1", "s2"}}, 400, httpx.CodeValidation, fields(map[string]string{"sku_ids": "not in cart: s1, s2"})},
		{"line problems", &domain.LineProblemsError{Problems: []domain.LineProblem{{SkuID: "s1", Reason: domain.ProblemOwnShop}}}, 409, httpx.CodeConflict,
			map[string]any{"items": []map[string]string{{"sku_id": "s1", "reason": "OWN_SHOP"}}}},
		{"out of stock", &app.OutOfStockError{Shortages: []domain.Shortage{{SkuID: "s1", Requested: 3, Available: 1}}}, 409, httpx.CodeOutOfStock,
			map[string]any{"shortages": []map[string]any{{"sku_id": "s1", "requested": int32(3), "available": int32(1)}}}},
		{"catalog rejected", app.ErrCatalogRejected, 409, httpx.CodeConflict, nil},
		{"catalog down", fmt.Errorf("%w: boom", app.ErrCatalogUnavailable), 503, httpx.CodeUpstreamUnavailable, nil},
		{"key in progress", app.ErrIdempotencyInProgress, 409, httpx.CodeConflict, nil},
		{"key reused", app.ErrIdempotencyKeyReused, 422, httpx.CodeIdempotencyKeyReused, nil},
		{"invalid transition", fmt.Errorf("%w: x", domain.ErrInvalidTransition), 409, httpx.CodeInvalidStateTransition, nil},
		{"concurrent update", app.ErrConcurrentUpdate, 409, httpx.CodeConflict, nil},
		{"checkout expired", app.ErrCheckoutExpired, 409, httpx.CodeConflict, nil},
		{"cursor", app.ErrInvalidCursor, 400, httpx.CodeValidation, fields(map[string]string{"cursor": "is invalid"})},
		{"app error passes through", httpx.NewError(httpx.CodeForbidden, "no"), 403, httpx.CodeForbidden, nil},
		{"anything else", errors.New("db down"), 500, httpx.CodeInternal, nil},
	}
	for _, tc := range cases {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			ae := toAppError(tc.err)
			assert.Equal(t, tc.status, ae.Status)
			assert.Equal(t, tc.code, ae.Code)
			if tc.details != nil {
				assert.Equal(t, tc.details, ae.Details)
			}
		})
	}
}
```
