- [x] **Step 4: Implement request helpers and error mapping**

`services/order/internal/adapter/http/request.go`:
```go
package httpadapter

import (
	"bytes"
	"io"
	"net/http"
	"strconv"
	"strings"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/pkg/authctx"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/pkg/httpx"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/app"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/domain"
)

const (
	headerIdempotencyKey = "Idempotency-Key"
	maxIdempotencyKey    = 100
	defaultPageLimit     = 20
	maxPageLimit         = 100
	maxCursorLen         = 500
)

// parseID returns the canonical (lower-case) form of a hyphenated UUID.
func parseID(s string) (string, bool) {
	if len(s) != 36 {
		return "", false
	}
	u, err := uuid.Parse(s)
	if err != nil {
		return "", false
	}
	return u.String(), true
}

// requireUUIDCaller rejects callers whose X-User-Id is not a UUID and stores
// its canonical form so ownership checks compare equal strings.
func requireUUIDCaller(next http.Handler) http.Handler {
	return http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		id, _ := authctx.FromContext(r.Context())
		canonical, ok := parseID(id.UserID)
		if !ok {
			httpx.WriteError(w, r, httpx.NewError(httpx.CodeUnauthenticated, "caller identity is not a valid user id"))
			return
		}
		id.UserID = canonical
		next.ServeHTTP(w, r.WithContext(authctx.WithIdentity(r.Context(), id)))
	})
}

func callerID(r *http.Request) string { return authctx.UserID(r.Context()) }

type reasonRequest struct {
	Reason string `json:"reason" validate:"required,max=200"`
}

type noteRequest struct {
	Reason string `json:"reason" validate:"max=200"`
}

// decodeReason reads a required cancel reason (1–200 characters, not blank).
func decodeReason(r *http.Request) (string, error) {
	var req reasonRequest
	if err := httpx.Decode(r, &req); err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	reason := strings.TrimSpace(req.Reason)
	if reason == "" {
		return "", httpx.ValidationError(map[string]string{"reason": "is required"})
	}
	return reason, nil
}

// decodeOptional decodes the body into dst unless the body is empty.
func decodeOptional(r *http.Request, dst any) error {
	body, err := io.ReadAll(io.LimitReader(r.Body, httpx.MaxBodyBytes+1))
	if err != nil {
		return httpx.ValidationError(map[string]string{"body": "could not be read"})
	}
	if len(bytes.TrimSpace(body)) == 0 {
		return nil
	}
	r.Body = io.NopCloser(bytes.NewReader(body))
	return httpx.Decode(r, dst)
}

// parseListInput reads status, limit and cursor query parameters.
func parseListInput(r *http.Request) (app.ListInput, error) {
	q := r.URL.Query()
	in := app.ListInput{Limit: defaultPageLimit, Cursor: q.Get("cursor")}
	fields := map[string]string{}
	if v := q.Get("limit"); v != "" {
		n, err := strconv.Atoi(v)
		if err != nil || n < 1 || n > maxPageLimit {
			fields["limit"] = "must be an integer between 1 and 100"
		} else {
			in.Limit = n
		}
	}
	if v := q.Get("status"); v != "" {
		st, ok := domain.ParseStatus(v)
		if !ok {
			fields["status"] = "must be a valid order status"
		} else {
			in.Status = &st
		}
	}
	if len(in.Cursor) > maxCursorLen {
		fields["cursor"] = "is invalid"
	}
	if len(fields) > 0 {
		return in, httpx.ValidationError(fields)
	}
	return in, nil
}
```

`services/order/internal/adapter/http/errors.go`:
```go
package httpadapter

import (
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"net/http"
	"strings"

	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/pkg/httpx"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/app"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/domain"
)

func writeError(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, err error) {
	httpx.WriteError(w, r, toAppError(err))
}

// toAppError maps use-case errors to API errors (WS0 §3 codes).
func toAppError(err error) *httpx.AppError {
	var (
		ae  *httpx.AppError
		sel *app.SelectionError
		lp  *domain.LineProblemsError
		oos *app.OutOfStockError
	)
	switch {
	case errors.As(err, &ae):
		if ae.Status == 0 {
			ae = httpx.NewError(ae.Code, ae.Message).WithDetails(ae.Details)
		}
		return ae
	case errors.Is(err, app.ErrNotFound):
		return httpx.NewError(httpx.CodeNotFound, "order not found")
	case errors.Is(err, app.ErrSkuNotFound):
		return httpx.NewError(httpx.CodeNotFound, "sku not found")
	case errors.Is(err, app.ErrSkuUnavailable):
		return httpx.NewError(httpx.CodeConflict, "sku is not available for sale")
	case errors.Is(err, app.ErrOwnShop):
		return httpx.NewError(httpx.CodeConflict, "cannot buy from your own shop")
	case errors.Is(err, app.ErrCartFull):
		return httpx.NewError(httpx.CodeConflict, fmt.Sprintf("cart cannot hold more than %d items", domain.MaxCartLines))
	case errors.Is(err, app.ErrInvalidQuantity):
		return httpx.ValidationError(map[string]string{"quantity": fmt.Sprintf("must be between %d and %d", domain.MinQuantity, domain.MaxQuantity)})
	case errors.Is(err, app.ErrEmptyCart):
		return httpx.ValidationError(map[string]string{"sku_ids": "no cart items to check out"})
	case errors.As(err, &sel):
		return httpx.ValidationError(map[string]string{"sku_ids": "not in cart: " + strings.Join(sel.NotInCart, ", ")})
	case errors.As(err, &lp):
		items := make([]map[string]string, len(lp.Problems))
		for i, p := range lp.Problems {
			items[i] = map[string]string{"sku_id": p.SkuID, "reason": p.Reason}
		}
		return httpx.NewError(httpx.CodeConflict, "some items cannot be purchased").WithDetails(map[string]any{"items": items})
	case errors.As(err, &oos):
		shortages := make([]map[string]any, len(oos.Shortages))
		for i, s := range oos.Shortages {
			shortages[i] = map[string]any{"sku_id": s.SkuID, "requested": s.Requested, "available": s.Available}
		}
		return httpx.NewError(httpx.CodeOutOfStock, "insufficient stock").WithDetails(map[string]any{"shortages": shortages})
	case errors.Is(err, app.ErrCatalogRejected):
		return httpx.NewError(httpx.CodeConflict, "catalog rejected the request")
	case errors.Is(err, app.ErrCatalogUnavailable):
		return httpx.NewError(httpx.CodeUpstreamUnavailable, "catalog service is unavailable").Wrap(err)
	case errors.Is(err, app.ErrIdempotencyInProgress):
		return httpx.NewError(httpx.CodeConflict, "a request with this Idempotency-Key is still in progress")
	case errors.Is(err, app.ErrIdempotencyKeyReused):
		return httpx.NewError(httpx.CodeIdempotencyKeyReused, "Idempotency-Key was already used with a different request")
	case errors.Is(err, domain.ErrInvalidTransition):
		return httpx.NewError(httpx.CodeInvalidStateTransition, err.Error())
	case errors.Is(err, app.ErrConcurrentUpdate):
		return httpx.NewError(httpx.CodeConflict, "order was modified concurrently; retry")
	case errors.Is(err, app.ErrCheckoutExpired):
		return httpx.NewError(httpx.CodeConflict, "checkout took too long and was cancelled; retry")
	case errors.Is(err, app.ErrInvalidCursor):
		return httpx.ValidationError(map[string]string{"cursor": "is invalid"})
	default:
		return httpx.NewError(httpx.CodeInternal, "internal server error").Wrap(err)
	}
}
```

`services/order/internal/adapter/http/dto.go`:
```go
package httpadapter

import (
	"time"

	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/app"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/domain"
)

// JSON shapes of api/openapi/order.yaml. Slices are always non-nil so empty
// collections encode as [] (the contract requires arrays).

type shopRefDTO struct {
	ID   string `json:"id"`
	Name string `json:"name"`
	Slug string `json:"slug"`
}

type cartItemDTO struct {
	SkuID       string  `json:"sku_id"`
	ProductID   string  `json:"product_id"`
	ProductName string  `json:"product_name"`
	VariantName string  `json:"variant_name"`
	ImageURL    *string `json:"image_url"`
	UnitPrice   int64   `json:"unit_price"`
	Quantity    int32   `json:"quantity"`
	Stock       int32   `json:"stock"`
	Available   bool    `json:"available"`
}

type cartShopDTO struct {
	Shop  shopRefDTO    `json:"shop"`
	Items []cartItemDTO `json:"items"`
}

type cartDTO struct {
	Shops    []cartShopDTO `json:"shops"`
	Subtotal int64         `json:"subtotal"`
}

type orderShopDTO struct {
	ID   string `json:"id"`
	Name string `json:"name"`
}

type orderItemDTO struct {
	SkuID       string  `json:"sku_id"`
	ProductID   string  `json:"product_id"`
	ProductName string  `json:"product_name"`
	VariantName string  `json:"variant_name"`
	ImageURL    *string `json:"image_url"`
	UnitPrice   int64   `json:"unit_price"`
	Quantity    int32   `json:"quantity"`
	LineTotal   int64   `json:"line_total"`
}

type historyDTO struct {
	From      *domain.Status `json:"from"`
	To        domain.Status  `json:"to"`
	ActorRole domain.Role    `json:"actor_role"`
	Note      *string        `json:"note"`
	CreatedAt time.Time      `json:"created_at"`
}

type orderDTO struct {
	ID              string         `json:"id"`
	CheckoutID      string         `json:"checkout_id"`
	Status          domain.Status  `json:"status"`
	BuyerID         string         `json:"buyer_id"`
	Shop            orderShopDTO   `json:"shop"`
	Items           []orderItemDTO `json:"items"`
	Subtotal        int64          `json:"subtotal"`
	ShippingFee     int64          `json:"shipping_fee"`
	Total           int64          `json:"total"`
	ShippingAddress domain.Address `json:"shipping_address"`
	PaymentMethod   string         `json:"payment_method"`
	CancelReason    *string        `json:"cancel_reason"`
	History         []historyDTO   `json:"history"`
	CreatedAt       time.Time      `json:"created_at"`
	UpdatedAt       time.Time      `json:"updated_at"`
}

type pageDTO struct {
	Items      []orderDTO `json:"items"`
	NextCursor *string    `json:"next_cursor"`
}

type checkoutResponseDTO struct {
	CheckoutID string     `json:"checkout_id"`
	Orders     []orderDTO `json:"orders"`
}

func toCartDTO(c domain.Cart) cartDTO {
	out := cartDTO{Shops: make([]cartShopDTO, 0, len(c.Shops)), Subtotal: c.Subtotal}
	for _, s := range c.Shops {
		shop := cartShopDTO{Shop: shopRefDTO{ID: s.Shop.ID, Name: s.Shop.Name, Slug: s.Shop.Slug}, Items: make([]cartItemDTO, 0, len(s.Items))}
		for _, it := range s.Items {
			shop.Items = append(shop.Items, cartItemDTO{
				SkuID: it.SkuID, ProductID: it.ProductID, ProductName: it.ProductName, VariantName: it.VariantName,
				ImageURL: it.ImageURL, UnitPrice: it.UnitPrice, Quantity: it.Quantity, Stock: it.Stock, Available: it.Available,
			})
		}
		out.Shops = append(out.Shops, shop)
	}
	return out
}

func toOrderDTO(o domain.Order) orderDTO {
	out := orderDTO{
		ID: o.ID, CheckoutID: o.CheckoutID, Status: o.Status, BuyerID: o.BuyerID,
		Shop: orderShopDTO{ID: o.ShopID, Name: o.ShopName}, Items: make([]orderItemDTO, 0, len(o.Items)),
		Subtotal: o.Subtotal, ShippingFee: o.ShippingFee, Total: o.Total, ShippingAddress: o.ShippingAddress,
		PaymentMethod: o.PaymentMethod, CancelReason: o.CancelReason, History: make([]historyDTO, 0, len(o.History)),
		CreatedAt: o.CreatedAt, UpdatedAt: o.UpdatedAt,
	}
	for _, it := range o.Items {
		out.Items = append(out.Items, orderItemDTO{
			SkuID: it.SkuID, ProductID: it.ProductID, ProductName: it.ProductName, VariantName: it.VariantName,
			ImageURL: it.ImageURL, UnitPrice: it.UnitPrice, Quantity: it.Quantity, LineTotal: it.LineTotal,
		})
	}
	for _, h := range o.History {
		out.History = append(out.History, historyDTO{From: h.From, To: h.To, ActorRole: h.ActorRole, Note: h.Note, CreatedAt: h.At})
	}
	return out
}

func toOrderDTOs(orders []domain.Order) []orderDTO {
	out := make([]orderDTO, 0, len(orders))
	for _, o := range orders {
		out = append(out, toOrderDTO(o))
	}
	return out
}

func toPageDTO(p app.Page) pageDTO {
	return pageDTO{Items: toOrderDTOs(p.Orders), NextCursor: p.NextCursor}
}
```

- [x] **Step 6: Implement the router and handlers**

`services/order/internal/adapter/http/router.go`:
```go
// Package httpadapter serves order-svc's REST API (api/openapi/order.yaml).
package httpadapter

import (
	"net/http"

	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/pkg/authctx"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/pkg/httpx"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/app"
)

// Services are the use cases exposed over HTTP.
type Services struct {
	Cart     *app.CartService
	Checkout *app.CheckoutService
	Orders   *app.OrderService
}

type handler struct{ s Services }

// NewRouter returns the /api routes. authctx.Middleware must run before it.
func NewRouter(s Services) http.Handler {
	h := &handler{s: s}
	user := func(f http.HandlerFunc) http.Handler { return authctx.RequireUser(requireUUIDCaller(f)) }
	seller := func(f http.HandlerFunc) http.Handler {
		return authctx.RequireRole(authctx.RoleSeller)(requireUUIDCaller(f))
	}

	mux := http.NewServeMux()
	mux.Handle("GET /api/cart", user(h.getCart))
	mux.Handle("PUT /api/cart/items/{sku_id}", user(h.setCartItem))
	mux.Handle("DELETE /api/cart/items/{sku_id}", user(h.removeCartItem))
	mux.Handle("POST /api/checkout", user(h.checkout))
	mux.Handle("GET /api/orders", user(h.listMyOrders))
	mux.Handle("GET /api/orders/{id}", user(h.getOrder))
	mux.Handle("POST /api/orders/{id}/cancel", user(h.cancelMyOrder))
	mux.Handle("POST /api/orders/{id}/complete", user(h.completeMyOrder))
	mux.Handle("GET /api/seller/orders", seller(h.listSellerOrders))
	mux.Handle("POST /api/seller/orders/{id}/{action}", seller(h.sellerAction))
	mux.Handle("/", httpx.NotFound())
	return mux
}
```

`services/order/internal/adapter/http/cart.go`:
```go
package httpadapter

import (
	"net/http"

	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/pkg/httpx"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/domain"
)

type cartItemRequest struct {
	Quantity *int32 `json:"quantity" validate:"required,min=1,max=99"`
}

func (h *handler) getCart(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	c, err := h.s.Cart.Get(r.Context(), callerID(r))
	if err != nil {
		writeError(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	httpx.WriteJSON(w, http.StatusOK, toCartDTO(c))
}

func (h *handler) setCartItem(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	skuID, ok := parseID(r.PathValue("sku_id"))
	if !ok {
		writeError(w, r, httpx.ValidationError(map[string]string{"sku_id": "must be a valid UUID"}))
		return
	}
	var req cartItemRequest
	if err := httpx.Decode(r, &req); err != nil {
		writeError(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	c, err := h.s.Cart.SetItem(r.Context(), callerID(r), skuID, *req.Quantity)
	if err != nil {
		writeError(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	httpx.WriteJSON(w, http.StatusOK, toCartDTO(c))
}

func (h *handler) removeCartItem(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	var (
		c   domain.Cart
		err error
	)
	if skuID, ok := parseID(r.PathValue("sku_id")); ok {
		c, err = h.s.Cart.RemoveItem(r.Context(), callerID(r), skuID)
	} else {
		c, err = h.s.Cart.Get(r.Context(), callerID(r)) // a non-UUID can never be in the cart
	}
	if err != nil {
		writeError(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	httpx.WriteJSON(w, http.StatusOK, toCartDTO(c))
}
```

`services/order/internal/adapter/http/checkout.go`:
```go
package httpadapter

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"net/http"
	"unicode/utf8"

	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/pkg/httpx"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/app"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/domain"
)

type addressRequest struct {
	FullName string `json:"full_name" validate:"required"`
	Phone    string `json:"phone" validate:"required"`
	Line1    string `json:"line1" validate:"required"`
	Ward     string `json:"ward" validate:"required"`
	District string `json:"district" validate:"required"`
	Province string `json:"province" validate:"required"`
}

type checkoutRequest struct {
	SkuIDs          *[]string       `json:"sku_ids" validate:"omitnil,min=1,dive,uuid"`
	ShippingAddress *addressRequest `json:"shipping_address" validate:"required"`
	PaymentMethod   string          `json:"payment_method" validate:"required,oneof=COD"`
}

func (h *handler) checkout(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	key := r.Header.Get(headerIdempotencyKey)
	if n := utf8.RuneCountInString(key); n < 1 || n > maxIdempotencyKey {
		writeError(w, r, httpx.ValidationError(map[string]string{headerIdempotencyKey: "header is required (1-100 characters)"}))
		return
	}
	var req checkoutRequest
	if err := httpx.Decode(r, &req); err != nil {
		writeError(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	a := req.ShippingAddress
	in := app.CheckoutInput{
		BuyerID: callerID(r), IdempotencyKey: key, PaymentMethod: req.PaymentMethod,
		Address: domain.Address{FullName: a.FullName, Phone: a.Phone, Line1: a.Line1, Ward: a.Ward, District: a.District, Province: a.Province},
	}
	if req.SkuIDs != nil {
		in.SkuIDs = make([]string, 0, len(*req.SkuIDs))
		for _, id := range *req.SkuIDs {
			canonical, _ := parseID(id) // validated by the uuid tag
			in.SkuIDs = append(in.SkuIDs, canonical)
		}
	}
	resp, err := h.s.Checkout.Checkout(r.Context(), in, encodeCheckout)
	if err != nil {
		writeError(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
	w.WriteHeader(resp.Status)
	_, _ = w.Write(resp.Body)
}

type errorEnvelope struct {
	Error errorPayload `json:"error"`
}

type errorPayload struct {
	Code    string         `json:"code"`
	Message string         `json:"message"`
	Details map[string]any `json:"details,omitempty"`
}

// encodeCheckout is the app.Encoder for checkout: 201 with the orders, or the
// standard error body for a business rejection.
func encodeCheckout(res app.CheckoutResult, err error) app.Response {
	if err != nil {
		ae := toAppError(err)
		body, _ := json.Marshal(errorEnvelope{Error: errorPayload{Code: ae.Code, Message: ae.Message, Details: ae.Details}})
		return app.Response{Status: ae.Status, Body: body}
	}
	body, _ := json.Marshal(checkoutResponseDTO{CheckoutID: res.CheckoutID, Orders: toOrderDTOs(res.Orders)})
	return app.Response{Status: http.StatusCreated, Body: body}
}
```

`services/order/internal/adapter/http/orders.go`:
```go
package httpadapter

import (
	"net/http"
	"strings"

	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/pkg/httpx"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/app"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/domain"
)

var sellerActions = map[string]domain.Action{
	"confirm": domain.ActionConfirm,
	"ship":    domain.ActionShip,
	"deliver": domain.ActionDeliver,
	"cancel":  domain.ActionCancel,
}

func (h *handler) listMyOrders(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	h.list(w, r, app.PartyBuyer)
}

func (h *handler) listSellerOrders(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	h.list(w, r, app.PartySeller)
}

func (h *handler) list(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, party app.Party) {
	in, err := parseListInput(r)
	if err != nil {
		writeError(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	in.Party, in.UserID = party, callerID(r)
	page, err := h.s.Orders.List(r.Context(), in)
	if err != nil {
		writeError(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	httpx.WriteJSON(w, http.StatusOK, toPageDTO(page))
}

func (h *handler) getOrder(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	id, ok := parseID(r.PathValue("id"))
	if !ok {
		writeError(w, r, app.ErrNotFound)
		return
	}
	o, err := h.s.Orders.Get(r.Context(), callerID(r), id)
	if err != nil {
		writeError(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	httpx.WriteJSON(w, http.StatusOK, toOrderDTO(o))
}

func (h *handler) cancelMyOrder(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	id, ok := parseID(r.PathValue("id"))
	if !ok {
		writeError(w, r, app.ErrNotFound)
		return
	}
	reason, err := decodeReason(r)
	if err != nil {
		writeError(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	h.act(w, r, app.ActInput{OrderID: id, Actor: domain.Actor{ID: callerID(r), Role: domain.RoleBuyer}, Action: domain.ActionCancel, Note: reason})
}

func (h *handler) completeMyOrder(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	id, ok := parseID(r.PathValue("id"))
	if !ok {
		writeError(w, r, app.ErrNotFound)
		return
	}
	h.act(w, r, app.ActInput{OrderID: id, Actor: domain.Actor{ID: callerID(r), Role: domain.RoleBuyer}, Action: domain.ActionComplete})
}

func (h *handler) sellerAction(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	action, ok := sellerActions[r.PathValue("action")]
	if !ok {
		writeError(w, r, httpx.NewError(httpx.CodeNotFound, "route not found"))
		return
	}
	id, ok := parseID(r.PathValue("id"))
	if !ok {
		writeError(w, r, app.ErrNotFound)
		return
	}
	var note string
	if action == domain.ActionCancel {
		reason, err := decodeReason(r)
		if err != nil {
			writeError(w, r, err)
			return
		}
		note = reason
	} else {
		var req noteRequest
		if err := decodeOptional(r, &req); err != nil {
			writeError(w, r, err)
			return
		}
		note = strings.TrimSpace(req.Reason)
	}
	h.act(w, r, app.ActInput{OrderID: id, Actor: domain.Actor{ID: callerID(r), Role: domain.RoleSeller}, Action: action, Note: note})
}

func (h *handler) act(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, in app.ActInput) {
	o, err := h.s.Orders.Act(r.Context(), in)
	if err != nil {
		writeError(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	httpx.WriteJSON(w, http.StatusOK, toOrderDTO(o))
}
```

```go
package main

import (
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"

	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/pkg/config"
)

func TestConfigDefaults(t *testing.T) {
	t.Setenv("SERVICE_NAME", "order")
	t.Setenv("CATALOG_GRPC_ADDR", "catalog:9082")
	cfg, err := config.Load[Config]()
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, "catalog:9082", cfg.CatalogGRPCAddr)
	assert.Equal(t, int64(30_000), cfg.ShippingFeeFlat)
	assert.Equal(t, 60*time.Second, cfg.SweeperInterval)
	assert.Equal(t, 2*time.Minute, cfg.PendingTimeout)
	assert.NoError(t, cfg.validate())
}

func TestConfigRequiresCatalogAddr(t *testing.T) {
	t.Setenv("SERVICE_NAME", "order")
	t.Setenv("CATALOG_GRPC_ADDR", "")
	_, err := config.Load[Config]()
	assert.ErrorContains(t, err, "CATALOG_GRPC_ADDR")
}

func TestConfigValidate(t *testing.T) {
	ok := Config{ShippingFeeFlat: 0, SweeperInterval: time.Second, PendingTimeout: time.Second}
	require.NoError(t, ok.validate())
	for name, mut := range map[string]func(*Config){
		"negative fee":  func(c *Config) { c.ShippingFeeFlat = -1 },
		"zero interval": func(c *Config) { c.SweeperInterval = 0 },
		"zero timeout":  func(c *Config) { c.PendingTimeout = 0 },
	} {
		c := ok
		mut(&c)
		assert.Error(t, c.validate(), name)
	}
}
```

```go
package main

import (
	"errors"
	"time"

	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/pkg/config"
)

// Config is the service configuration (WS0 base + WS3 §6 variables).
type Config struct {
	config.Base
	CatalogGRPCAddr string        `env:"CATALOG_GRPC_ADDR,required,notEmpty"`
	ShippingFeeFlat int64         `env:"SHIPPING_FEE_FLAT" envDefault:"30000"`
	SweeperInterval time.Duration `env:"SWEEPER_INTERVAL" envDefault:"60s"`
	PendingTimeout  time.Duration `env:"PENDING_TIMEOUT" envDefault:"2m"`
}

func (c Config) validate() error {
	switch {
	case c.ShippingFeeFlat < 0:
		return errors.New("SHIPPING_FEE_FLAT must be >= 0")
	case c.SweeperInterval <= 0:
		return errors.New("SWEEPER_INTERVAL must be > 0")
	case c.PendingTimeout <= 0:
		return errors.New("PENDING_TIMEOUT must be > 0")
	}
	return nil
}
```
Delete the `Config` type (and its comment) from `main.go` — it now lives in `config.go`.

- [x] **Step 4: Implement bootstrap**

`services/order/internal/bootstrap/bootstrap.go`:
```go
// Package bootstrap wires order-svc's adapters, use cases, HTTP API and
// background workers. cmd/server and the end-to-end tests share it.
package bootstrap

import (
	"context"
	"log/slog"
	"net/http"
	"sync"
	"time"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"
	"github.com/redis/go-redis/v9"
	"google.golang.org/grpc"

	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/pkg/outbox"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/pkg/server"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/adapter/catalog"
	httpadapter "github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/adapter/http"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/adapter/postgres"
	redisadapter "github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/adapter/redis"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/app"
)

// Config holds the tunables.
type Config struct {
	ShippingFeeFlat int64
	SweeperInterval time.Duration
	PendingTimeout  time.Duration
	Catalog         catalog.Config
	RelayInterval   time.Duration // 0 = outbox default (500ms)
}

// Producer publishes outbox messages and reports broker reachability
// (*kafkax.Producer).
type Producer interface {
	outbox.Publisher
	Ping(ctx context.Context) error
}

// Deps are the live connections.
type Deps struct {
	Pool        *pgxpool.Pool
	Redis       *redis.Client
	CatalogConn grpc.ClientConnInterface
	Producer    Producer
	Logger      *slog.Logger     // nil = slog.Default()
	Now         func() time.Time // nil = UTC wall clock
}

// App is the wired service.
type App struct {
	API     http.Handler // /api routes; wrap with authctx.Middleware
	Relay   *outbox.Relay
	Sweeper *app.Sweeper
	checks  map[string]server.Check
}

func New(cfg Config, d Deps) *App {
	if d.Logger == nil {
		d.Logger = slog.Default()
	}
	if d.Now == nil {
		d.Now = func() time.Time { return time.Now().UTC().Truncate(time.Microsecond) }
	}
	store := postgres.NewStore(d.Pool)
	carts := redisadapter.NewCartStore(d.Redis)
	cat := catalog.New(d.CatalogConn, cfg.Catalog)
	return &App{
		API: httpadapter.NewRouter(httpadapter.Services{
			Cart: app.NewCartService(carts, cat, d.Logger),
			Checkout: app.NewCheckoutService(store, carts, cat, app.CheckoutConfig{
				ShippingFee: cfg.ShippingFeeFlat, Now: d.Now, Logger: d.Logger,
			}),
			Orders: app.NewOrderService(store, d.Now),
		}),
		Relay: outbox.NewRelay(d.Pool, d.Producer, outbox.RelayConfig{Interval: cfg.RelayInterval, Logger: d.Logger}),
		Sweeper: app.NewSweeper(store, cat, app.SweeperConfig{
			Interval: cfg.SweeperInterval, PendingTimeout: cfg.PendingTimeout, Now: d.Now, Logger: d.Logger,
		}),
		checks: map[string]server.Check{
			"postgres": d.Pool.Ping,
			"redis":    func(ctx context.Context) error { return d.Redis.Ping(ctx).Err() },
			"kafka":    d.Producer.Ping,
		},
	}
}

// ReadinessChecks are the /readyz dependencies: Postgres, Redis, Kafka.
func (a *App) ReadinessChecks() map[string]server.Check { return a.checks }

// RunWorkers runs the outbox relay and the sweeper until ctx is cancelled.
func (a *App) RunWorkers(ctx context.Context) {
	var wg sync.WaitGroup
	wg.Add(2)
	go func() { defer wg.Done(); _ = a.Relay.Run(ctx) }()
	go func() { defer wg.Done(); _ = a.Sweeper.Run(ctx) }()
	wg.Wait()
}
```

- [x] **Step 5: Wire `cmd/server/main.go`**

Replace the body of `run()` in `services/order/cmd/server/main.go` from the `pool, err := pgxx.NewPool(...)` block onwards so the whole function reads:
```go
func run() error {
	ctx, stop := signal.NotifyContext(context.Background(), os.Interrupt, syscall.SIGTERM)
	defer stop()

	cfg, err := config.Load[Config]()
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if err := cfg.validate(); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	logger := logx.New(os.Stdout, cfg.LogLevel).With("service", cfg.ServiceName)
	slog.SetDefault(logger)

	shutdownOtel, err := otelx.Setup(ctx, cfg.ServiceName, cfg.OTLPEndpoint)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer func() { _ = shutdownOtel(context.Background()) }()

	if err := pgxx.Migrate(cfg.DatabaseURL, migrations.FS); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("migrate: %w", err)
	}
	pool, err := pgxx.NewPool(ctx, cfg.DatabaseURL)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer pool.Close()

	redisOpts, err := redis.ParseURL(cfg.RedisURL)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("parse REDIS_URL: %w", err)
	}
	rdb := redis.NewClient(redisOpts)
	defer func() { _ = rdb.Close() }()

	producer, err := kafkax.NewProducer(cfg.KafkaBrokers)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer producer.Close()

	catalogConn, err := grpcx.Dial(cfg.CatalogGRPCAddr)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("dial catalog: %w", err)
	}
	defer func() { _ = catalogConn.Close() }()

	svc := bootstrap.New(bootstrap.Config{
		ShippingFeeFlat: cfg.ShippingFeeFlat, SweeperInterval: cfg.SweeperInterval,
		PendingTimeout: cfg.PendingTimeout, Catalog: catalog.DefaultConfig(),
	}, bootstrap.Deps{Pool: pool, Redis: rdb, CatalogConn: catalogConn, Producer: producer, Logger: logger})

	workersCtx, stopWorkers := context.WithCancel(ctx)
	workersDone := make(chan struct{})
	go func() { defer close(workersDone); svc.RunWorkers(workersCtx) }()
	defer func() { stopWorkers(); <-workersDone }()

	grpcSrv, _ := grpcx.NewServer()
	return server.Run(ctx, server.Config{
		HTTP: &http.Server{
			Addr:              cfg.HTTPAddr,
			Handler:           otelx.HTTPHandler(newHandler(logger, svc.ReadinessChecks(), svc.API), cfg.ServiceName),
			ReadHeaderTimeout: 10 * time.Second,
		},
		GRPC:            grpcSrv,
		GRPCAddr:        cfg.GRPCAddr,
		ShutdownTimeout: cfg.ShutdownTimeout,
		Logger:          logger,
	})
}
```
Add imports `github.com/redis/go-redis/v9`, `github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/pkg/kafkax`, `github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/adapter/catalog`, `github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/bootstrap` (goimports will group them). The deferred `stopWorkers(); <-workersDone` runs before the connection closers (LIFO), so workers stop before Kafka/Redis/Postgres close.

```go
// Package itest runs order-svc end to end over HTTP — real Postgres, Redis
// and Kafka (testcontainers) plus the catalog fake — validating every request
// and response against api/openapi/order.yaml and every published event
// against api/events.
package itest

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"net/http"
	"net/http/httptest"
	"path/filepath"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"
	"github.com/redis/go-redis/v9"
	"github.com/santhosh-tekuri/jsonschema/v6"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"
	"github.com/twmb/franz-go/pkg/kgo"

	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/pkg/authctx"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/pkg/event"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/pkg/kafkax"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/pkg/pgxx"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/pkg/testutil"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/adapter/catalog"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/adapter/catalog/catalogfake"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/bootstrap"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/migrations"
)

const (
	orderSpec       = "../../../../api/openapi/order.yaml"
	eventsDir       = "../../../../api/events"
	pendingTimeout  = 2 * time.Second
	sweeperInterval = 500 * time.Millisecond
)

var ctx = context.Background()

var address = map[string]string{
	"full_name": "Nguyễn Văn A", "phone": "0900000000", "line1": "1 Lê Lợi",
	"ward": "Bến Nghé", "district": "Quận 1", "province": "TP.HCM",
}

func newID() string { return uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()).String() }

// TestOrderAPI runs every scenario against one set of containers.
func TestOrderAPI(t *testing.T) {
	h := newHarness(t)
	t.Run("cart CRUD", h.testCartCRUD)
	t.Run("cart rejects own-shop, unknown and inactive SKUs", h.testCartRejects)
	t.Run("cart flags a SKU that became unavailable", h.testCartFlagsUnavailable)
	t.Run("cart answers 503 while catalog is down", h.testCartCatalogDown)
	t.Run("checkout from two shops", h.testCheckoutTwoShops)
	t.Run("checkout is all-or-nothing on shortage", h.testCheckoutOutOfStock)
	t.Run("checkout 503 frees the idempotency key", h.testCheckoutCatalogDownThenRetry)
	t.Run("checkout with an empty cart", h.testCheckoutEmptyCart)
	// Task 15 adds the lifecycle, sweeper, pagination and readiness scenarios here.
	t.Run("every published event matches its schema", h.testEventsValid)
}

type caller struct{ id, roles string }

func newBuyer() caller                       { return caller{id: newID(), roles: "buyer"} }
func sellerOf(shop catalogfake.Shop) caller { return caller{id: shop.SellerUserID, roles: "buyer,seller"} }

type harness struct {
	app      *bootstrap.App
	pool     *pgxpool.Pool
	fake     *catalogfake.Fake
	events   *eventLog
	baseURL  string
	contract *http.Client // validates against order.yaml
	raw      *http.Client // for deliberately invalid requests
}

func newHarness(t *testing.T) *harness {
	dsn := testutil.Postgres(t)
	require.NoError(t, pgxx.Migrate(dsn, migrations.FS))
	pool, err := pgxx.NewPool(ctx, dsn)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	t.Cleanup(pool.Close)

	opts, err := redis.ParseURL(testutil.Redis(t))
	require.NoError(t, err)
	rdb := redis.NewClient(opts)
	t.Cleanup(func() { _ = rdb.Close() })

	brokers := testutil.Kafka(t)
	testutil.CreateTopic(t, brokers, event.TopicOrderEvents, 3)
	producer, err := kafkax.NewProducer(brokers)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	t.Cleanup(producer.Close)

	fake := catalogfake.New()
	a := bootstrap.New(bootstrap.Config{
		ShippingFeeFlat: 30_000, SweeperInterval: sweeperInterval, PendingTimeout: pendingTimeout,
		Catalog:       catalog.Config{Timeout: 300 * time.Millisecond, Retries: 2, Backoff: 10 * time.Millisecond},
		RelayInterval: 50 * time.Millisecond,
	}, bootstrap.Deps{Pool: pool, Redis: rdb, CatalogConn: fake.Dial(t), Producer: producer})

	wctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(ctx)
	done := make(chan struct{})
	go func() { defer close(done); a.RunWorkers(wctx) }()
	t.Cleanup(func() { cancel(); <-done })

	srv := httptest.NewServer(authctx.Middleware(a.API))
	t.Cleanup(srv.Close)

	return &harness{
		app: a, pool: pool, fake: fake, events: newEventLog(t, brokers), baseURL: srv.URL,
		contract: &http.Client{Transport: testutil.ContractTransport(t, orderSpec, nil)},
		raw:      &http.Client{},
	}
}

// try sends a request and returns status and body; safe off the test goroutine.
func (h *harness) try(client *http.Client, c caller, method, path string, body io.Reader, headers ...string) (int, []byte, error) {
	req, err := http.NewRequest(method, h.baseURL+path, body)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, nil, err
	}
	if body != nil {
		req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
	}
	if c.id != "" {
		req.Header.Set(authctx.HeaderUserID, c.id)
		req.Header.Set(authctx.HeaderUserRoles, c.roles)
	}
	for i := 0; i+1 < len(headers); i += 2 {
		req.Header.Set(headers[i], headers[i+1])
	}
	resp, err := client.Do(req)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, nil, err // includes contract violations
	}
	defer func() { _ = resp.Body.Close() }()
	b, err := io.ReadAll(resp.Body)
	return resp.StatusCode, b, err
}

// do sends a contract-validated request with an optional JSON body.
func (h *harness) do(t *testing.T, c caller, method, path string, body any, headers ...string) (int, []byte) {
	t.Helper()
	var r io.Reader
	if body != nil {
		b, err := json.Marshal(body)
		require.NoError(t, err)
		r = bytes.NewReader(b)
	}
	status, b, err := h.try(h.contract, c, method, path, r, headers...)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	return status, b
}

// doRaw sends a request that bypasses contract validation.
func (h *harness) doRaw(t *testing.T, c caller, method, path, body string, headers ...string) (int, []byte) {
	t.Helper()
	var r io.Reader
	if body != "" {
		r = bytes.NewReader([]byte(body))
	}
	status, b, err := h.try(h.raw, c, method, path, r, headers...)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	return status, b
}

func decode[T any](t *testing.T, b []byte) T {
	t.Helper()
	var v T
	require.NoError(t, json.Unmarshal(b, &v), string(b))
	return v
}

func (h *harness) addToCart(t *testing.T, c caller, skuID string, q int) {
	t.Helper()
	status, body := h.do(t, c, http.MethodPut, "/api/cart/items/"+skuID, map[string]int{"quantity": q})
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, status, string(body))
}

func checkoutBody(skuIDs ...string) map[string]any {
	b := map[string]any{"shipping_address": address, "payment_method": "COD"}
	if len(skuIDs) > 0 {
		b["sku_ids"] = skuIDs
	}
	return b
}

func (h *harness) checkout(t *testing.T, c caller, key string, body map[string]any) (int, []byte) {
	t.Helper()
	return h.do(t, c, http.MethodPost, "/api/checkout", body, "Idempotency-Key", key)
}

// Response mirrors (only the fields the scenarios inspect).
type cartResp struct {
	Shops []struct {
		Shop  struct{ ID, Name, Slug string } `json:"shop"`
		Items []struct {
			SkuID     string `json:"sku_id"`
			UnitPrice int64  `json:"unit_price"`
			Quantity  int32  `json:"quantity"`
			Stock     int32  `json:"stock"`
			Available bool   `json:"available"`
		} `json:"items"`
	} `json:"shops"`
	Subtotal int64 `json:"subtotal"`
}

type orderResp struct {
	ID           string  `json:"id"`
	CheckoutID   string  `json:"checkout_id"`
	Status       string  `json:"status"`
	BuyerID      string  `json:"buyer_id"`
	Total        int64   `json:"total"`
	CancelReason *string `json:"cancel_reason"`
	Shop         struct{ ID, Name string } `json:"shop"`
	History      []struct {
		From      *string `json:"from"`
		To        string  `json:"to"`
		ActorRole string  `json:"actor_role"`
		Note      *string `json:"note"`
		CreatedAt string  `json:"created_at"`
	} `json:"history"`
}

type checkoutResp struct {
	CheckoutID string      `json:"checkout_id"`
	Orders     []orderResp `json:"orders"`
}

type pageResp struct {
	Items      []orderResp `json:"items"`
	NextCursor *string     `json:"next_cursor"`
}

type errResp struct {
	Error struct {
		Code    string         `json:"code"`
		Message string         `json:"message"`
		Details map[string]any `json:"details"`
	} `json:"error"`
}

// eventLog consumes order.events from the start, validating every message.
type eventLog struct {
	mu      sync.Mutex
	byOrder map[string][]event.Envelope
	total   int
	invalid []string
}

func newEventLog(t *testing.T, brokers []string) *eventLog {
	c := jsonschema.NewCompiler()
	c.AssertFormat()
	schemas := map[event.Type]*jsonschema.Schema{}
	for typ, file := range map[event.Type]string{
		event.TypeOrderCreated:       "order-created.schema.json",
		event.TypeOrderStatusChanged: "order-status-changed.schema.json",
		event.TypeOrderCancelled:     "order-cancelled.schema.json",
	} {
		sch, err := c.Compile(filepath.Join(eventsDir, file))
		require.NoError(t, err)
		schemas[typ] = sch
	}
	cl, err := kgo.NewClient(kgo.SeedBrokers(brokers...), kgo.ConsumeTopics(event.TopicOrderEvents),
		kgo.ConsumeResetOffset(kgo.NewOffset().AtStart()))
	require.NoError(t, err)

	l := &eventLog{byOrder: map[string][]event.Envelope{}}
	pctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(ctx)
	done := make(chan struct{})
	go func() {
		defer close(done)
		for {
			fetches := cl.PollFetches(pctx)
			if pctx.Err() != nil {
				return
			}
			fetches.EachRecord(func(r *kgo.Record) { l.add(schemas, r) })
		}
	}()
	t.Cleanup(func() { cancel(); <-done; cl.Close() })
	return l
}

func (l *eventLog) add(schemas map[event.Type]*jsonschema.Schema, r *kgo.Record) {
	l.mu.Lock()
	defer l.mu.Unlock()
	l.total++
	env, err := event.Decode(r.Value)
	if err != nil {
		l.invalid = append(l.invalid, fmt.Sprintf("decode %s: %v", r.Key, err))
		return
	}
	inst, err := jsonschema.UnmarshalJSON(bytes.NewReader(r.Value))
	sch := schemas[env.EventType]
	switch {
	case err != nil || sch == nil:
		l.invalid = append(l.invalid, fmt.Sprintf("%s %s: no schema or bad JSON: %v", env.EventType, r.Key, err))
	default:
		if err := sch.Validate(inst); err != nil {
			l.invalid = append(l.invalid, fmt.Sprintf("%s %s: %v", env.EventType, r.Key, err))
		}
	}
	l.byOrder[string(r.Key)] = append(l.byOrder[string(r.Key)], env)
}

// waitTypes waits until orderID's events have the given types, in order.
func (l *eventLog) waitTypes(t *testing.T, orderID string, want ...event.Type) []event.Envelope {
	t.Helper()
	var got []event.Envelope
	require.Eventually(t, func() bool {
		l.mu.Lock()
		defer l.mu.Unlock()
		got = append([]event.Envelope(nil), l.byOrder[orderID]...)
		if len(got) != len(want) {
			return false
		}
		for i := range want {
			if got[i].EventType != want[i] {
				return false
			}
		}
		return true
	}, 15*time.Second, 50*time.Millisecond, "events of %s: want %v", orderID, want)
	return got
}
```

- [x] **Step 8: Write the cart and checkout scenarios**

`services/order/internal/itest/cart_checkout_test.go`:
```go
package itest

import (
	"net/http"
	"testing"

	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"
	"google.golang.org/grpc/codes"

	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/pkg/event"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/adapter/catalog/catalogfake"
)

func (h *harness) testCartCRUD(t *testing.T) {
	b, shop := newBuyer(), catalogfake.NewShop("Cart Shop")
	s1 := h.fake.AddSku(shop, 100_000, 3)
	s2 := h.fake.AddSku(shop, 50_000, 10)

	status, body := h.do(t, b, http.MethodGet, "/api/cart", nil)
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, status)
	assert.JSONEq(t, `{"shops":[],"subtotal":0}`, string(body))

	status, body = h.do(t, b, http.MethodPut, "/api/cart/items/"+s1.GetSkuId(), map[string]int{"quantity": 5})
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, status, string(body))
	cart := decode[cartResp](t, body)
	require.Len(t, cart.Shops, 1)
	assert.Equal(t, shop.Slug, cart.Shops[0].Shop.Slug)
	assert.Equal(t, int32(5), cart.Shops[0].Items[0].Quantity)
	assert.Equal(t, int32(3), cart.Shops[0].Items[0].Stock)
	assert.Equal(t, int64(500_000), cart.Subtotal)

	h.addToCart(t, b, s2.GetSkuId(), 1)
	h.addToCart(t, b, s1.GetSkuId(), 2)
	status, body = h.do(t, b, http.MethodDelete, "/api/cart/items/"+s2.GetSkuId(), nil)
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, status)
	cart = decode[cartResp](t, body)
	assert.Len(t, cart.Shops[0].Items, 1)
	assert.Equal(t, int64(200_000), cart.Subtotal)

	status, _ = h.doRaw(t, b, http.MethodDelete, "/api/cart/items/not-a-uuid", "")
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, status)
}

func (h *harness) testCartRejects(t *testing.T) {
	shop := catalogfake.NewShop("Own Shop")
	own := h.fake.AddSku(shop, 10_000, 5)
	status, body := h.do(t, sellerOf(shop), http.MethodPut, "/api/cart/items/"+own.GetSkuId(), map[string]int{"quantity": 1})
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusConflict, status)
	assert.Equal(t, "CONFLICT", decode[errResp](t, body).Error.Code)

	status, body = h.do(t, newBuyer(), http.MethodPut, "/api/cart/items/"+newID(), map[string]int{"quantity": 1})
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusNotFound, status)
	assert.Equal(t, "NOT_FOUND", decode[errResp](t, body).Error.Code)

	off := h.fake.AddSku(catalogfake.NewShop("Inactive"), 10_000, 5)
	h.fake.SetAvailable(off.GetSkuId(), false)
	status, _ = h.do(t, newBuyer(), http.MethodPut, "/api/cart/items/"+off.GetSkuId(), map[string]int{"quantity": 1})
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusConflict, status)
}

func (h *harness) testCartFlagsUnavailable(t *testing.T) {
	b := newBuyer()
	s := h.fake.AddSku(catalogfake.NewShop("Flag"), 10_000, 5)
	h.addToCart(t, b, s.GetSkuId(), 2)
	h.fake.SetAvailable(s.GetSkuId(), false)

	status, body := h.do(t, b, http.MethodGet, "/api/cart", nil)
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, status)
	cart := decode[cartResp](t, body)
	assert.False(t, cart.Shops[0].Items[0].Available)
	assert.Zero(t, cart.Subtotal)
}

func (h *harness) testCartCatalogDown(t *testing.T) {
	b := newBuyer()
	h.addToCart(t, b, h.fake.AddSku(catalogfake.NewShop("Down"), 10_000, 5).GetSkuId(), 1)
	h.fake.FailNext(catalogfake.GetSkus, codes.Unavailable, 3)
	status, body := h.do(t, b, http.MethodGet, "/api/cart", nil)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusServiceUnavailable, status)
	assert.Equal(t, "UPSTREAM_UNAVAILABLE", decode[errResp](t, body).Error.Code)
}

func (h *harness) testCheckoutTwoShops(t *testing.T) {
	b, shopA, shopB := newBuyer(), catalogfake.NewShop("Shop A"), catalogfake.NewShop("Shop B")
	a := h.fake.AddSku(shopA, 120_000, 10)
	bb := h.fake.AddSku(shopB, 45_000, 10)
	h.addToCart(t, b, a.GetSkuId(), 2)
	h.addToCart(t, b, bb.GetSkuId(), 1)
	reserves := h.fake.Calls(catalogfake.ReserveStock)

	status, body := h.checkout(t, b, "ck-1", checkoutBody())
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusCreated, status, string(body))
	res := decode[checkoutResp](t, body)
	require.Len(t, res.Orders, 2)
	assert.Equal(t, reserves+2, h.fake.Calls(catalogfake.ReserveStock))
	totals := map[string]int64{}
	for _, o := range res.Orders {
		assert.Equal(t, "PENDING_CONFIRMATION", o.Status)
		assert.Equal(t, res.CheckoutID, o.CheckoutID)
		totals[o.Shop.ID] = o.Total
		evs := h.events.waitTypes(t, o.ID, event.TypeOrderCreated, event.TypeOrderStatusChanged)
		created, err := event.DecodeData[event.OrderCreated](evs[0])
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.Equal(t, o.Total, created.Total)
		assert.Equal(t, res.CheckoutID, created.CheckoutID)
	}
	assert.Equal(t, map[string]int64{shopA.ID: 270_000, shopB.ID: 75_000}, totals)

	status, body = h.do(t, b, http.MethodGet, "/api/cart", nil)
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, status)
	assert.JSONEq(t, `{"shops":[],"subtotal":0}`, string(body), "purchased lines leave the cart")

	status, body = h.do(t, b, http.MethodGet, "/api/orders", nil)
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, status)
	assert.Len(t, decode[pageResp](t, body).Items, 2)

	// Same key, same body → identical response, no new reservations.
	status2, body2 := h.checkout(t, b, "ck-1", checkoutBody())
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusCreated, status2)
	assert.JSONEq(t, string(res.mustJSON(t)), string(decode[checkoutResp](t, body2).mustJSON(t)))
	assert.Equal(t, reserves+2, h.fake.Calls(catalogfake.ReserveStock))

	// Same key, different body → 422.
	other := checkoutBody()
	other["shipping_address"] = map[string]string{"full_name": "B", "phone": "1", "line1": "x", "ward": "w", "district": "d", "province": "p"}
	status3, body3 := h.checkout(t, b, "ck-1", other)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, status3)
	assert.Equal(t, "IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED", decode[errResp](t, body3).Error.Code)
}

func (h *harness) testCheckoutOutOfStock(t *testing.T) {
	b := newBuyer()
	a := h.fake.AddSku(catalogfake.NewShop("Plenty"), 10_000, 10)
	short := h.fake.AddSku(catalogfake.NewShop("Scarce"), 10_000, 1)
	h.addToCart(t, b, a.GetSkuId(), 2)
	h.addToCart(t, b, short.GetSkuId(), 3)

	status, body := h.checkout(t, b, "oos", checkoutBody())
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusConflict, status, string(body))
	e := decode[errResp](t, body)
	assert.Equal(t, "OUT_OF_STOCK", e.Error.Code)
	assert.Equal(t, []any{map[string]any{"sku_id": short.GetSkuId(), "requested": 3.0, "available": 1.0}}, e.Error.Details["shortages"])

	status, body = h.do(t, b, http.MethodGet, "/api/orders?status=CANCELLED", nil)
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, status)
	page := decode[pageResp](t, body)
	require.Len(t, page.Items, 2)
	for _, o := range page.Items {
		assert.Equal(t, "OUT_OF_STOCK", *o.CancelReason)
		assert.Contains(t, h.fake.ReleasedOrders(), o.ID)
		h.events.waitTypes(t, o.ID, event.TypeOrderStatusChanged, event.TypeOrderCancelled)
	}
	assert.Equal(t, int32(10), h.fake.Stock(a.GetSkuId()))
	status, body = h.do(t, b, http.MethodGet, "/api/cart", nil)
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, status)
	assert.Len(t, decode[cartResp](t, body).Shops, 2, "the cart is kept")
}

func (h *harness) testCheckoutCatalogDownThenRetry(t *testing.T) {
	b := newBuyer()
	s := h.fake.AddSku(catalogfake.NewShop("Flaky"), 10_000, 10)
	h.addToCart(t, b, s.GetSkuId(), 1)
	h.fake.FailReserveForSkus(codes.Unavailable, s.GetSkuId())

	status, body := h.checkout(t, b, "flaky", checkoutBody())
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusServiceUnavailable, status)
	assert.Equal(t, "UPSTREAM_UNAVAILABLE", decode[errResp](t, body).Error.Code)

	h.fake.FailReserveForSkus(codes.OK, s.GetSkuId())
	status, body = h.checkout(t, b, "flaky", checkoutBody())
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusCreated, status, string(body))
}

func (h *harness) testCheckoutEmptyCart(t *testing.T) {
	status, body := h.checkout(t, newBuyer(), "empty", checkoutBody())
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusBadRequest, status)
	assert.Equal(t, "VALIDATION_ERROR", decode[errResp](t, body).Error.Code)
}

func (h *harness) testEventsValid(t *testing.T) {
	h.events.mu.Lock()
	defer h.events.mu.Unlock()
	assert.Positive(t, h.events.total)
	assert.Empty(t, h.events.invalid)
}
```

```go
package itest

import (
	"net/http"
	"net/http/httptest"
	"strings"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"

	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/pkg/event"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/pkg/server"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/adapter/catalog"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/adapter/catalog/catalogfake"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/adapter/postgres"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/app"
	"github.com/ngoxuanchien/e-commerce/services/order/internal/domain"
)

// placeOrder checks out one SKU of a new shop and returns the order and shop.
func (h *harness) placeOrder(t *testing.T, b caller) (orderResp, catalogfake.Shop) {
	t.Helper()
	shop := catalogfake.NewShop("Life " + newID()[30:])
	s := h.fake.AddSku(shop, 50_000, 10)
	h.addToCart(t, b, s.GetSkuId(), 1)
	status, body := h.checkout(t, b, newID(), checkoutBody(s.GetSkuId()))
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusCreated, status, string(body))
	return decode[checkoutResp](t, body).Orders[0], shop
}

func (h *harness) act(t *testing.T, c caller, path string, body any) (int, orderResp) {
	t.Helper()
	status, b := h.do(t, c, http.MethodPost, path, body)
	if status != http.StatusOK {
		return status, orderResp{}
	}
	return status, decode[orderResp](t, b)
}

func (h *harness) testLifecycle(t *testing.T) {
	b := newBuyer()
	o, shop := h.placeOrder(t, b)
	s := sellerOf(shop)
	for _, step := range []struct {
		who  caller
		path string
		body any
		want string
	}{
		{s, "/api/seller/orders/" + o.ID + "/confirm", nil, "CONFIRMED"},
		{s, "/api/seller/orders/" + o.ID + "/ship", map[string]string{"reason": "GHN-123"}, "SHIPPING"},
		{s, "/api/seller/orders/" + o.ID + "/deliver", nil, "DELIVERED"},
		{b, "/api/orders/" + o.ID + "/complete", nil, "COMPLETED"},
	} {
		status, got := h.act(t, step.who, step.path, step.body)
		require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, status, step.path)
		assert.Equal(t, step.want, got.Status)
	}
	for _, who := range []caller{b, s} {
		status, body := h.do(t, who, http.MethodGet, "/api/orders/"+o.ID, nil)
		require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, status)
		got := decode[orderResp](t, body)
		require.Len(t, got.History, 6)
		assert.Equal(t, "GHN-123", *got.History[3].Note)
	}
	h.events.waitTypes(t, o.ID, event.TypeOrderCreated,
		event.TypeOrderStatusChanged, event.TypeOrderStatusChanged, event.TypeOrderStatusChanged,
		event.TypeOrderStatusChanged, event.TypeOrderStatusChanged)
}

func (h *harness) testBuyerCannotCancelConfirmed(t *testing.T) {
	b := newBuyer()
	o, shop := h.placeOrder(t, b)
	status, _ := h.act(t, sellerOf(shop), "/api/seller/orders/"+o.ID+"/confirm", nil)
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, status)

	status, body := h.do(t, b, http.MethodPost, "/api/orders/"+o.ID+"/cancel", map[string]string{"reason": "đổi ý"})
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusConflict, status)
	assert.Equal(t, "INVALID_STATE_TRANSITION", decode[errResp](t, body).Error.Code)

	status, got := h.act(t, sellerOf(shop), "/api/seller/orders/"+o.ID+"/cancel", map[string]string{"reason": "hết hàng"})
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, status)
	assert.Equal(t, "CANCELLED", got.Status)
	evs := h.events.waitTypes(t, o.ID, event.TypeOrderCreated, event.TypeOrderStatusChanged,
		event.TypeOrderStatusChanged, event.TypeOrderStatusChanged, event.TypeOrderCancelled)
	c, err := event.DecodeData[event.OrderCancelled](evs[4])
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, event.OrderCancelled{OrderID: o.ID, Reason: "hết hàng", CancelledBy: "seller"}, c)
}

func (h *harness) testStrangers(t *testing.T) {
	o, _ := h.placeOrder(t, newBuyer())
	other := sellerOf(catalogfake.NewShop("Other"))
	status, _ := h.do(t, other, http.MethodPost, "/api/seller/orders/"+o.ID+"/confirm", nil)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusNotFound, status)
	status, _ = h.do(t, other, http.MethodGet, "/api/orders/"+o.ID, nil)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusNotFound, status)
	status, _ = h.do(t, newBuyer(), http.MethodGet, "/api/seller/orders", nil)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusForbidden, status)
}

func (h *harness) testConcurrentConfirmCancel(t *testing.T) {
	for range 10 {
		b := newBuyer()
		o, shop := h.placeOrder(t, b)
		type call struct {
			c    caller
			path string
		}
		calls := []call{{sellerOf(shop), "/api/seller/orders/" + o.ID + "/confirm"}, {b, "/api/orders/" + o.ID + "/cancel"}}
		statuses := make([]int, len(calls))
		errs := make([]error, len(calls))
		start := make(chan struct{})
		var wg sync.WaitGroup
		for i, c := range calls {
			wg.Add(1)
			go func() {
				defer wg.Done()
				<-start
				statuses[i], _, errs[i] = h.try(h.contract, c.c, http.MethodPost, c.path, strings.NewReader(`{"reason":"đổi ý"}`))
			}()
		}
		close(start)
		wg.Wait()
		for _, err := range errs {
			require.NoError(t, err)
		}
		assert.ElementsMatch(t, []int{http.StatusOK, http.StatusConflict}, statuses)
	}
}

func (h *harness) testUppercaseIDs(t *testing.T) {
	b := newBuyer()
	o, _ := h.placeOrder(t, b)
	upper := caller{id: strings.ToUpper(b.id), roles: b.roles}
	status, body := h.doRaw(t, upper, http.MethodGet, "/api/orders/"+strings.ToUpper(o.ID), "")
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, status, string(body))
	assert.Equal(t, o.ID, decode[orderResp](t, body).ID)
}

func (h *harness) testPagination(t *testing.T) {
	b := newBuyer()
	shops := []catalogfake.Shop{catalogfake.NewShop("P1"), catalogfake.NewShop("P2"), catalogfake.NewShop("P3")}
	for _, s := range shops {
		h.addToCart(t, b, h.fake.AddSku(s, 10_000, 5).GetSkuId(), 1)
	}
	status, body := h.checkout(t, b, "page", checkoutBody())
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusCreated, status, string(body))

	status, body = h.do(t, b, http.MethodGet, "/api/orders?limit=2", nil)
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, status)
	first := decode[pageResp](t, body)
	require.Len(t, first.Items, 2)
	require.NotNil(t, first.NextCursor)

	status, body = h.do(t, b, http.MethodGet, "/api/orders?limit=2&cursor="+*first.NextCursor, nil)
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, status)
	second := decode[pageResp](t, body)
	require.Len(t, second.Items, 1)
	assert.Nil(t, second.NextCursor)
	seen := map[string]bool{first.Items[0].ID: true, first.Items[1].ID: true, second.Items[0].ID: true}
	assert.Len(t, seen, 3, "no overlap between pages")

	status, body = h.do(t, sellerOf(shops[0]), http.MethodGet, "/api/seller/orders?status=PENDING_CONFIRMATION", nil)
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, status)
	assert.Len(t, decode[pageResp](t, body).Items, 1)
}

func (h *harness) testSweeperCancelsCrashedCheckout(t *testing.T) {
	// Simulate a crash between Tx A and Tx C: a PENDING order holding stock.
	s := h.fake.AddSku(catalogfake.NewShop("Crash"), 10_000, 5)
	cat := catalog.New(h.fake.Dial(t), catalog.DefaultConfig())
	skus, err := cat.GetSkus(ctx, []string{s.GetSkuId()})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	orders, err := domain.BuildOrders(domain.CheckoutParams{
		CheckoutID: newID(), BuyerID: newID(), Lines: []domain.Line{{SkuID: s.GetSkuId(), Quantity: 2}}, Skus: skus,
		ShippingFee: 30_000, Address: domain.Address{FullName: "A", Phone: "1", Line1: "l", Ward: "w", District: "d", Province: "p"},
		PaymentMethod: domain.PaymentCOD, Now: time.Now().UTC().Truncate(time.Microsecond), NewID: newID,
	})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	o := orders[0]
	store := postgres.NewStore(h.pool)
	require.NoError(t, store.InTx(ctx, func(tx app.Tx) error { return tx.InsertOrder(ctx, o) }))
	require.NoError(t, cat.ReserveStock(ctx, o.ID, o.StockLines()))

	buyer := caller{id: o.BuyerID, roles: "buyer"}
	var got orderResp
	require.Eventually(t, func() bool {
		status, body, err := h.try(h.contract, buyer, http.MethodGet, "/api/orders/"+o.ID, nil)
		if err != nil || status != http.StatusOK {
			return false
		}
		got = decode[orderResp](t, body)
		return got.Status == "CANCELLED"
	}, pendingTimeout+sweeperInterval+2*time.Second, 50*time.Millisecond)

	cancelledAt, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339Nano, got.History[len(got.History)-1].CreatedAt)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	lifetime := cancelledAt.Sub(o.CreatedAt)
	assert.LessOrEqual(t, lifetime, pendingTimeout+sweeperInterval+300*time.Millisecond, "PENDING_TIMEOUT + one interval (+ slack)")
	assert.Equal(t, "CHECKOUT_TIMEOUT", *got.CancelReason)
	assert.Equal(t, int32(5), h.fake.Stock(s.GetSkuId()))
	h.events.waitTypes(t, o.ID, event.TypeOrderStatusChanged, event.TypeOrderCancelled)
}

func (h *harness) testReadiness(t *testing.T) {
	checks := h.app.ReadinessChecks()
	names := make([]string, 0, len(checks))
	for name := range checks {
		names = append(names, name)
	}
	assert.ElementsMatch(t, []string{"postgres", "redis", "kafka"}, names)
	rec := httptest.NewRecorder()
	server.Readyz(checks).ServeHTTP(rec, httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodGet, "/readyz", nil))
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, rec.Code, rec.Body.String())
}
```
